    VERSION 1.0 CLASS
    BEGIN
      MultiUse = -1
    END
    Attribute VB_Name = "clsPerpendicularLocate"
    Attribute VB_GlobalNameSpace = False
    Attribute VB_Creatable = False
    Attribute VB_PredeclaredId = False
    Attribute VB_Exposed = False
    Option Explicit
    Implements ILocateCommandEvents
    Implements IPrimitiveCommandEvents

    Private mTarget As Long
    Private mReverse As Boolean
    Private mPreviewLength As Double
    Private mPlacement As Long
    Private mFirstDistance As Double
    Private mFollowWithEnd As Boolean
    Private mFinishing As Boolean

    Public Sub ConfigurePick(ByVal target As Long, ByVal reverse As Boolean, ByVal previewLength As Double, _
            ByVal placement As Long, ByVal firstDistance As Double, ByVal followWithEnd As Boolean)
        mTarget = target
        mReverse = reverse
        mPreviewLength = previewLength
        mPlacement = placement
        mFirstDistance = firstDistance
        mFollowWithEnd = followWithEnd
    End Sub

    Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_Start()
        Dim criteria As LocateCriteria
        Set criteria = CommandState.CreateLocateCriteria(False)
        CommandState.SetLocateCriteria criteria
        ShowCommand "Perpendicular Line Generator"
        ShowPrompt "Identify horizontal geometry, then accept. Reset cancels."
    End Sub

    Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_LocateFilter(ByVal Element As Element, Point As Point3d, Accepted As Boolean)
        On Error GoTo Rejected
        Accepted = False
        If Element.ModelReference.IsAttachment Then Exit Sub
        If Not Element.ModelReference.IsActive Then Exit Sub
        Select Case Element.Type
            Case msdElementTypeLine, msdElementTypeLineString, msdElementTypeArc, _
                    msdElementTypeBsplineCurve, msdElementTypeComplexString
                Accepted = True
            Case Else
                ShowPrompt "Unsupported type. Native civil spirals need a Civil SDK evaluator. Select a standard linear element."
        End Select
        Exit Sub
    Rejected:
        Accepted = False
        ShowPrompt Err.Description
    End Sub

    Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_Accept(ByVal Element As Element, Point As Point3d, ByVal View As View)
        On Error GoTo Failed
        If Not PLGBeginAlignment(Element) Then CommandState.StartLocate New clsPerpendicularLocate
        Exit Sub
    Failed:
        MsgBox Err.Description, vbExclamation, "Perpendicular Line Generator"
        CommandState.StartDefaultCommand
    End Sub

    Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_LocateReset()
        CommandState.StartDefaultCommand
    End Sub

    Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_LocateFailed()
        ShowPrompt "No supported element found. Select a line, line string, arc, B-spline, or complex string. Reset cancels."
    End Sub

    Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_Dynamics(Point As Point3d, ByVal View As View, ByVal DrawMode As MsdDrawingMode)
    End Sub

    Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_Cleanup()
    End Sub

    Private Sub IPrimitiveCommandEvents_Start()
        Dim direction As String
        If mReverse Then direction = "Reverse" Else direction = "Forward"
        ShowCommand "Perpendicular Line Generator - Range"
        If mTarget = 1 Then
            ShowPrompt "Pick START (" & direction & ") on the alignment. Move for preview, data point to accept. Reset opens settings."
        Else
            ShowPrompt "Pick END after the start in the chosen direction. Move for preview, data point to accept. Reset opens settings."
        End If
        CommandState.EnableAccuSnap
        CommandState.StartDynamics
    End Sub

    Private Sub DrawLimit(ByVal distance As Double, ByVal drawMode As MsdDrawingMode)
        Dim p As Point3d, t As Point3d, a As Point3d, b As Point3d, preview As LineElement
        PLGEvaluate distance, mReverse, p, t
        PLGEndpoints p, t, mPreviewLength, mPlacement, a, b
        Set preview = CreateLineElement2(Nothing, a, b)
        preview.Redraw drawMode
    End Sub

    Private Sub IPrimitiveCommandEvents_Dynamics(Point As Point3d, ByVal View As View, ByVal DrawMode As MsdDrawingMode)
        Dim distance As Double, anchor As Point3d, prompt As String
        On Error GoTo Failed
        PLGRequire PLGPickContextValid(), "The active model changed. Reset or start the tool again."
        distance = PLGNearestDistance(Point, mReverse, anchor)
        If mTarget = 2 Then DrawLimit mFirstDistance, DrawMode
        DrawLimit distance, DrawMode
        If mTarget = 1 Then prompt = "START" Else prompt = "END"
        prompt = prompt & " geometry distance: " & Format(distance, "0.000") & " master units. Click to accept; Reset opens settings."
        If mTarget = 2 And distance <= mFirstDistance + PLGDistanceTolerance() Then
            prompt = "END must follow START in the chosen direction. Reset to change Direction or choose the full alignment."
        End If
        ShowPrompt prompt
        Exit Sub
    Failed:
        ShowPrompt Err.Description
    End Sub

    Private Sub IPrimitiveCommandEvents_DataPoint(Point As Point3d, ByVal View As View)
        Dim distance As Double, anchor As Point3d, p As Point3d, t As Point3d
        On Error GoTo Failed
        PLGRequire PLGPickContextValid(), "The active model changed. Reset or start the tool again."
        distance = PLGNearestDistance(Point, mReverse, anchor)
        PLGEvaluate distance, mReverse, p, t
        If mTarget = 2 Then
            PLGRequire distance > mFirstDistance + PLGDistanceTolerance(), _
                "END must follow START in the chosen direction. Pick farther along, or Reset to change Direction."
        End If
        FinishPick True, distance
        Exit Sub
    Failed:
        ShowPrompt Err.Description
    End Sub

    Private Sub FinishPick(ByVal accepted As Boolean, ByVal distance As Double)
        StopPick
        PLGFinishRangePick mTarget, accepted, distance, mFollowWithEnd
    End Sub

    Public Sub StopPick()
        mFinishing = True
        CommandState.StartDefaultCommand
    End Sub

    Private Sub IPrimitiveCommandEvents_Reset()
        FinishPick False, 0#
    End Sub

    Private Sub IPrimitiveCommandEvents_Cleanup()
        If Not mFinishing Then PLGCloseSession
    End Sub

    Private Sub IPrimitiveCommandEvents_Keyin(ByVal Keyin As String)
    End Sub
    # Geometry and Bentley API notes

    ## Verification sources

    The primary VBA reference used during implementation was the help file delivered
    with the installed ORD 2026.00:

    ```text
    C:\Program Files\Bentley\OpenRoads Designer 2026.00\OpenRoadsDesigner\MicroStationVBA.chm
    ```

    Signatures and interface membership were also inspected by read-only reflection
    of its delivered `Assemblies\Bentley.Interop.MicroStationDGN.dll`. No ORD process
    was launched for that inspection. In the VBA Object Browser, use **F2** and search
    the Bentley library for the members below. In the help file, search their names.
    The HTML topic identifiers below make the reference repeatable after extracting
    the help with Windows HTML Help's `-decompile` option.

    | Member / topic | Use |
    | --- | --- |
    | `CommandState.StartLocate` and `ILocateCommandEvents` / `usobjilocatecommandevents.htm`, `ussmpLocate.htm` | Graphical locate, filtering, accept, reset and cleanup signatures |
    | `CommandState.StartPrimitive`, `IPrimitiveCommandEvents` / `usobjiprimitivecommandevents.htm`, `ussmpPrimitive.htm` | Start/end data-point callbacks, Reset, key-in and command cleanup signatures |
    | `CommandState.StartDynamics`, `EnableAccuSnap`; `Element.Redraw(DrawMode)` / `usmthstartdynamics.htm`, `usevtdynamics.htm` | Cursor-driven temporary cross-line previews; only Redraw inside Dynamics, never AddElement |
    | `Element.IsTraversableElement`, `AsTraversableElement` | Test/access traversable geometry |
    | `TraversableElement.PointAtDistance(Distance)` / `usmthpointatdistance.htm` | Original geometry endpoints for validation |
    | Typed `LineElement.Length`, `ArcElement.Length`, `BsplineCurveElement.Length`, `ComplexStringElement.Length` | Original element length; **`TraversableElement` does not have `Length`** |
    | `Element.AsVertexList.GetVertices` / `usmthgetvertices.htm` | Actual line string's zero-based vertices; there is no `AsLineStringElement` cast |
    | `ComplexStringElement.GetSubElements` / `usmthgetsubelements.htm` | Enumerate component copies without changing the source |
    | `BsplineCurve.FromElement(Element)` / `usmthfromelement.htm` | Convert supported line/arc/spline components to evaluable geometry objects |
    | `BsplineCurve.ComputeCurveLength([StartParameter, EndParameter, IgnoreZ])` / `usmthcomputecurvelength.htm` | Full/partial curve length; not chord length |
    | `BsplineCurve.EvaluatePointAtDistance(Parameter, DistanceFromStart, [StartParameter, Tolerance])` / `usmthevaluatepointatdistance.htm` | Return point and **output** curve parameter from a distance; explicit relative integration tolerance `1e-12` |
    | `BsplineCurve.EvaluatePointTangent(Tangent, Parameter)` / `usmthevaluatepointtangent.htm` | Return point and **output** unnormalized analytic tangent |
    | `BsplineCurve.ComputeMinimumDistance(ClosestPoint, ClosestParameter, FromPoint, Rotation, [Tolerance])` / `usmthcomputeminimumdistance.htm` | Closest point and **output** parameter; identity rotation computes XY distance, ignoring cursor Z |
    | `BsplineCurve.EvaluatePoint`, `Pole`, `PolesCount`, `Knot`, `KnotsCount`, `Closed` | Independent point check, horizontal plane and knot checks |
    | `Application.CreateLineElement2(Template, StartPoint, EndPoint)` / `usmthcreatelineelement2.htm` | Prepare independent straight output elements using active settings with `Template = Nothing` |
    | `Element.Level` / `usprolevel.htm` | A real `Level` object can be assigned only **after** insertion; newly created elements get active level automatically |
    | `ActiveSettings.Level`, `Color`, `LineStyle`, `LineWeight` | Capture settings on Generate; `LineStyle` is an object assigned with `Set` |
    | `ModelReference.AddElement`, `Element.Rewrite`, `ModelReference.RemoveElement` | Write and best-effort rollback |
    | `ModelReference.IsReadOnly`, `Level.ElementAccess` | Preflight writable output; `DesignFile.IsReadOnly` is not used |
    | `ModelReference.IsActive`, `IsAttachment`, `Type` | Check the captured model is current, reject attachments and exclude sheet models |
    | `ModelReference.MasterUnit`, `SubUnit`, `UORsPerMasterUnit`; `MeasurementUnit.Label` | Working units and computational resolution |

    The optional self-test also uses documented `CreateLineElement1`,
    `CreateArcElement2`, `CreateComplexStringElement1` with a `ChainableElement()`
    array, `CreateBsplineCurveElement1`, and `BsplineCurve.SetPoles/Order/SetKnots`.
    No deprecated distance-to-parameter method, MDL pointer/DLL declaration, C
    expression, guessed `PointAtStation` method, or Civil COM object is used.

    The load/run syntax follows Bentley's
    [Getting Started with Visual Basic](https://bentleysystems.service-now.com/community?id=kb_article_view&sysparm_article=KB0109946)
    and [Running a VBA Routine by Keyin](https://bentleysystems.service-now.com/community?id=kb_article_view&sysparm_article=KB0039735).

    ## Geometry policies

    These are **implementation safeguards, not roadway design criteria**:

    - Distance tolerance `T = max(2 / UORsPerMasterUnit, sourceLength * 1e-10)`, in
      master units. It accounts for two model resolution units plus an accumulated
      numerical length budget. It is used for endpoint matching,
      length residuals, and coincidence with range ends/joins. The code does not
      round placement distances to displayed precision.
    - All component poles must share the geometry origin's Z within
      `max(1e-12, abs(originZ)*1e-14)` master units, a floating-point plane-check
      budget. This is deliberately tighter than the distance tolerance so small
      slopes or accumulated component elevation changes are not mistaken for a
      horizontal alignment.
    - `EvaluatePointAtDistance` receives a **relative integration error request** of
      `1e-12`. Each result is checked by recomputing partial curve length from zero
      to the returned parameter. This is a cross-check within Bentley's curve engine,
      not an independent proof of the engine. The returned point is also compared
      with `EvaluatePointTangent`'s point.
    - B-spline parameter is **not** generally a fraction of length. The code never
      uses `distance / totalLength` as the parameter.
    - XY tangents are normalized and rotated left to `(-ty, tx)`. Centered endpoints
      are `p +/- length/2 * normal`. Left-only uses `p + length * normal` to `p`;
      right-only uses `p` to `p - length * normal`. All endpoints retain `p.Z`.
    - Components keep their stored order; a component is traversed backwards if its
      end matches the preceding endpoint. No gap is bridged and no source is reversed.
      Ordering that cannot be reconciled with the parent's endpoints is rejected.
    - At requested component joins, endpoint tangents must agree within a unit-vector
      difference of `1e-6` (approximately one microradian). A non-smooth corner has
      no unique mathematical tangent, so placement there fails the whole preflight.
    - Internal spline knots are checked on both sides using Bentley's derivatives;
      no numerical derivative or nearest-segment tangent is used for a curved
      component. Side probes use `1e-8` of the neighboring parameter span, with a
      further factor of `0.01` for point-limit checks. Very small spans, extreme
      curvature, or near-degenerate splines may be conservatively rejected. These
      probes are discontinuity guards, not a certification of a civil spiral fit.
    - Graphical picking calls `ComputeMinimumDistance` for every component with
      `Matrix3dIdentity`, checks the returned point against `EvaluatePoint`, and checks
      its reported distance against explicit XY distance. Cumulative path distance
      is the preceding components' lengths plus `ComputeCurveLength(0, parameter)`,
      with component/global reversal applied. It is never the distance from the
      cursor to an endpoint, a parameter fraction, or a snapped polyline surrogate.
    - All component candidates within `T` of the best XY distance are checked for
      conflicting chain distances. Shared endpoints with the same distance are
      allowed; crossings/overlaps with distinct distances are rejected. Bentley's
      method returns only one minimum per individual B-spline. Multiple equally
      close locations **within** one B-spline cannot be resolved by this interface;
      use unambiguous cursor positions or type a distance. Its optional tolerance
      argument is documented as **unused for B-splines**, so none is supplied and no
      tighter closest-point convergence is claimed from that parameter.
    - Dynamics draws the moving limit and, during end selection, the fixed start.
      It passes the event's `DrawMode` unchanged to `Redraw`. It creates only temporary
      in-memory lines and does not mutate the data-point argument or source geometry.
      Accepted picks stop the primitive command before resuming the modal form or
      next pick. Cleanup caused by another command releases the session without
      opening a modal dialog over that command.
    - Inputs/geometry below these budgets are rejected. Do not weaken safeguards to
      make an unexplained failure pass. Investigate the original geometry and ORD
      build against the analytical fixtures first.

    ## Status prompts and the native Tool Settings panel

    `ShowPrompt` and `ShowCommand` are documented to write to MicroStation's status
    area, explaining the lower-left range text. They do not populate Tool Settings.
    The installed ORD 2026 VBA `Application`/`CommandState` interfaces checked here
    expose no Tool Settings form-attachment API.

    Bentley's [Adding Windows Forms to Add-ins](https://bentleysystems.service-now.com/community?id=kb_article_view&sysparm_article=KB0012740)
    documents native Tool Settings integration through a managed form derived from
    `Bentley.MstnPlatformNET.WinForms.Adapter`, using
    `AttachToToolSettings(MyAddin.Addin)`. That is a .NET add-in interface, not a
    method available on this VBA `UserForm`.

    Bentley also documents [modeless VBA forms](https://bentleysystems.service-now.com/community?id=kb_article_view&sysparm_article=KB0028347).
    A floating modeless VBA panel could show the current step/distance near the
    right-hand tools while mouse picking continues. It would remain a separate
    window. Native panel integration needs the SDK add-in route. Neither UI change
    has been implemented; the choice is pending. Simply changing the existing
    modal `.Show` to `vbModeless` would require changing its session callbacks too.

    ## Native ORD geometry and the documented alternative

    Bentley documents the civil schema separately from basic DGN geometry in
    [Understanding Geometry model](https://docs.bentley.com/LiveContent/web/OpenRoads%20Designer%20SDK%20Help-v1/en/GUID-E99BC4FE-A0C9-4C20-8756-E8104911C083.html).
    The official examples obtain a managed `ConsensusConnection`, a
    `GeometricModel`, and its native `Alignment` objects. This supports the decision
    to keep native alignment/stationing work in an ORD-specific SDK layer rather
    than assuming VBA's ordinary DGN elements expose it.

    Bentley's [ORD SDK Developer Guide](https://bentleysystems.service-now.com/community?id=kb_article_view&sysparm_article=KB0012549)
    documents managed and unmanaged development, building examples, running custom
    projects inside ORD, and debugging managed projects. The
    [Civil Programming index](https://bentleysystems.service-now.com/community?id=kb_article_view&sysparm_article=KB0012546)
    links the SDK downloads, installation, examples and geometry-model documentation.

    For native spiral/station-equation support beyond the verified exposed VBA
    geometry, a **separate Bentley Civil SDK/.NET add-in is required by this
    implementation's support contract**. A future evaluator must read the native
    alignment's actual horizontal primitive definitions, use a validated native
    distance/tangent traversal, and separately translate displayed stationing and
    station equations. It must be built and tested against the installed ORD 2026
    SDK version. No claim is made here that one particular undocumented managed
    method or a generic curve conversion handles all native spirals.

    The absence of a spiral-specific evaluator in this VBA release does not prove
    that every native alignment is inaccessible. It means the tool cannot establish
    the native definition's accuracy through these ordinary VBA interfaces alone.
    If the selected element is a B-spline representation, the macro evaluates that
    **B-spline**, with no guarantee about its fit to an underlying clothoid. If it
    is a line string, it evaluates that **line string**, with no claim that its chord
    length equals the native spiral length. The manual support gate must establish
    equivalence before use for civil geometry.
    ✓ • 0ms

## Activity

    $ sed -n '1,210p' PerpendicularLineGenerator/PerpendicularLineGeneratorMain.bas; sed -n '1,120p' PerpendicularLineGenerator/modPerpendicularGeometry.bas; cat PerpendicularLineGenerator/TEST_PLAN.md
    Attribute VB_Name = "PerpendicularLineGeneratorMain"
    Option Explicit

    ' Key-in: VBA RUN [PerpendicularLineGenerator]PerpendicularLineGeneratorMain.Main
    Private mSource As Element
    Private mModel As ModelReference
    Private mSessionActive As Boolean
    Private mStatus As String
    Private mPicker As clsPerpendicularLocate

    Public Sub Main()
        On Error GoTo Failed
        If mSessionActive Then Exit Sub
        PLGRequire ActiveModelReference.Type <> msdModelTypeSheet, "Open the alignment's design/drawing model before running this tool. Sheet models are excluded."
        CommandState.StartLocate New clsPerpendicularLocate
        Exit Sub
    Failed:
        MsgBox Err.Description, vbExclamation, "Perpendicular Line Generator"
        CommandState.StartDefaultCommand
    End Sub

    Public Function PLGBeginAlignment(source As Element) As Boolean
        Dim reason As String
        On Error GoTo Failed
        If mSessionActive Then Exit Function
        PLGRequire ActiveModelReference.Type <> msdModelTypeSheet, "Open the alignment's design/drawing model before running this tool. Sheet models are excluded."
        PLGRequire source.ModelReference.IsActive And Not source.ModelReference.IsAttachment, _
            "Select geometry in the active model. Reference attachments are not supported."
        If Not PLGLoadPath(source, reason) Then
            MsgBox reason, vbExclamation, "Unsupported alignment"
            Exit Function
        End If
        Set mSource = source
        Set mModel = ActiveModelReference
        mSessionActive = True
        mStatus = reason
        frmPerpendicularLines.RefreshContext mStatus
        BeginRangePick 1, True
        PLGBeginAlignment = True
        Exit Function
    Failed:
        MsgBox Err.Description, vbExclamation, "Perpendicular Line Generator"
        PLGCloseSession
    End Function

    Public Sub PLGCloseSession()
        Set mPicker = Nothing
        mSessionActive = False
        Set mSource = Nothing
        Set mModel = Nothing
        PLGClearPath
    End Sub

    Public Function PLGPickContextValid() As Boolean
        On Error GoTo Done
        If Not mSessionActive Then Exit Function
        If mModel Is Nothing Then Exit Function
        PLGPickContextValid = mModel.IsActive
    Done:
    End Function

    Private Sub ShowSettingsDialog()
        Dim target As Long
        On Error GoTo Failed
        PLGRequire PLGPickContextValid(), "The active model changed. Run the tool again."
        frmPerpendicularLines.PrepareDialog mStatus
        frmPerpendicularLines.Show vbModal
        target = frmPerpendicularLines.RequestedPick
        If target > 0 Then
            BeginRangePick target, False
        Else
            PLGCloseSession
        End If
        Exit Sub
    Failed:
        MsgBox Err.Description, vbExclamation, "Perpendicular Line Generator"
        PLGCloseSession
    End Sub

    Private Sub BeginRangePick(ByVal target As Long, ByVal followWithEnd As Boolean)
        Dim reverse As Boolean, previewLength As Double, placement As Long, firstDistance As Double
        Dim errorText As String, stopError As Long
        On Error GoTo Failed
        PLGRequire PLGPickContextValid(), "The active model changed. Run the tool again."
        frmPerpendicularLines.ReadPickSettings target, reverse, previewLength, placement, firstDistance
        Set mPicker = New clsPerpendicularLocate
        mPicker.ConfigurePick target, reverse, previewLength, placement, firstDistance, followWithEnd
        CommandState.StartPrimitive mPicker
        Exit Sub
    Failed:
        errorText = Err.Description
        On Error Resume Next
        Err.Clear
        If Not mPicker Is Nothing Then mPicker.StopPick Else CommandState.StartDefaultCommand
        stopError = Err.Number
        Set mPicker = Nothing
        On Error GoTo 0
        If stopError <> 0 Then
            PLGCloseSession
            MsgBox errorText & vbCrLf & "The picker could not be stopped. Start another ORD command before retrying.", vbExclamation, "Perpendicular Line Generator"
            Exit Sub
        End If
        MsgBox errorText, vbExclamation, "Perpendicular Line Generator"
        If PLGPickContextValid() Then ShowSettingsDialog Else PLGCloseSession
    End Sub

    Public Sub PLGFinishRangePick(ByVal target As Long, ByVal accepted As Boolean, _
            ByVal distance As Double, ByVal followWithEnd As Boolean)
        On Error GoTo Failed
        Set mPicker = Nothing
        PLGRequire PLGPickContextValid(), "The active model changed. Run the tool again."
        If accepted Then frmPerpendicularLines.SetPickedDistance target, distance
        If accepted And target = 1 And followWithEnd Then
            BeginRangePick 2, False
        Else
            ShowSettingsDialog
        End If
        Exit Sub
    Failed:
        MsgBox Err.Description, vbExclamation, "Perpendicular Line Generator"
        PLGCloseSession
    End Sub

    Public Function PLGUnitsDescription() As String
        Dim master As MeasurementUnit, subUnit As MeasurementUnit
        master = ActiveModelReference.MasterUnit
        subUnit = ActiveModelReference.SubUnit
        PLGUnitsDescription = "Master: " & master.Label & " | Sub: " & subUnit.Label
    End Function

    Public Function PLGGenerate(ByVal first As Double, ByVal last As Double, ByVal spacing As Double, _
            ByVal totalLength As Double, ByVal placement As Long, ByVal reverse As Boolean, _
            ByVal includeFirst As Boolean, ByVal includeLast As Boolean) As String
        Dim level As Level, style As LineStyle, color As Long, weight As Long
        Dim lines As New Collection, line As LineElement, currentSource As Element
        Dim distances() As Double, n As Long, i As Long, attempted As Long, remaining As Long
        Dim p As Point3d, t As Point3d, a As Point3d, b As Point3d
        Dim reason As String, originalError As String
        On Error GoTo Failed
        PLGRequire Not mSource Is Nothing, "Select an alignment first."
        PLGRequire mModel.IsActive, "The active model changed. Cancel and run the macro again."
        PLGRequire Not ActiveModelReference.IsReadOnly, "The active model/DGN is read-only."
        ' Capture settings on Generate; do not reuse the values shown when the dialog opened.
        Set level = ActiveSettings.Level
        Set style = ActiveSettings.LineStyle
        color = ActiveSettings.Color
        weight = ActiveSettings.LineWeight
        PLGRequire level.ElementAccess = msdLevelElementAccessAll, _
            "The active level must allow modification/deletion so failed writes can be rolled back. Choose an unlocked writable level."
        Set currentSource = mModel.GetElementByID(mSource.ID)
        If Not PLGLoadPath(currentSource, reason) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2605, , reason
        n = PLGBuildDistances(first, last, spacing, includeFirst, includeLast, distances)
        For i = 1 To n
            PLGEvaluate distances(i), reverse, p, t
            PLGEndpoints p, t, totalLength, placement, a, b
            Set line = CreateLineElement2(Nothing, a, b)
            line.Color = color
            line.LineWeight = weight
            Set line.LineStyle = style
            PLGRequire Abs(line.Length - totalLength) <= PLGDistanceTolerance(), "A prepared line has the wrong length."
            lines.Add line
        Next i
        ' All geometry and all LineElements now exist in memory; only this loop writes.
        For i = 1 To lines.Count
            Set line = lines(i)
            attempted = i
            ActiveModelReference.AddElement line
            ' Bentley forbids assigning a Level object before AddElement.
            Set line.Level = level
            line.Rewrite
        Next i
        PLGGenerate = "Generated " & CStr(n) & " perpendicular lines at " & Format(spacing, "0.00") & _
            " master-unit spacing on level " & level.Name & "." & vbCrLf & PLGUnitsDescription()
        If includeLast Then PLGGenerate = PLGGenerate & vbCrLf & "The ending line may have a shorter final spacing."
        Exit Function
    Failed:
        originalError = Err.Description
        ' Remove only this run's elements; never issue a broad Undo command.
        On Error Resume Next
        For i = attempted To 1 Step -1
            Err.Clear
            Set line = lines(i)
            If DLongToString(line.ID) <> "0" Then mModel.RemoveElement line
            If Err.Number <> 0 Then remaining = remaining + 1
        Next i
        On Error GoTo 0
        If remaining > 0 Then
            originalError = originalError & vbCrLf & "Rollback could not remove " & CStr(remaining) & _
                " element(s). Use ordinary Undo and inspect the model before retrying."
        ElseIf attempted > 0 Then
            originalError = originalError & vbCrLf & "This run's added elements were removed."
        Else
            originalError = originalError & vbCrLf & "No elements were added."
        End If
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2604, "Perpendicular Lines", originalError
    End Function
    Attribute VB_Name = "modPerpendicularGeometry"
    Option Explicit

    ' Geometry distances and coordinates are in active-model master units.
    ' Bentley ORD 2026 MicroStationVBA.chm: BsplineCurve.FromElement,
    ' ComputeCurveLength, EvaluatePointAtDistance, EvaluatePointTangent.
    ' shortcut: evaluate exposed VBA geometry only; use a Civil SDK add-in when native spirals or station equations are required.
    Public Const PLG_MAX_LINES As Long = 10000
    Private Const MAX_PARTS As Long = 10000
    Private Const ANGLE_TOL As Double = 0.000001
    Private mCurve() As BsplineCurve
    Private mLength() As Double
    Private mBackwards() As Boolean
    Private mCount As Long
    Private mTotal As Double
    Private mTolerance As Double
    Private mPlaneZ As Double
    Private mPlaneTolerance As Double

    Public Sub PLGClearPath()
        Erase mCurve
        Erase mLength
        Erase mBackwards
        mCount = 0
        mTotal = 0#
    End Sub

    Public Function PLGPathLength() As Double
        PLGPathLength = mTotal
    End Function

    Public Function PLGDistanceTolerance() As Double
        PLGDistanceTolerance = mTolerance
    End Function

    Public Sub PLGRequire(ByVal condition As Boolean, ByVal message As String)
        If Not condition Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2601, "Perpendicular Lines", message
    End Sub

    Public Function PLGPointDistance(a As Point3d, b As Point3d) As Double
        PLGPointDistance = Sqr((a.X - b.X) ^ 2 + (a.Y - b.Y) ^ 2 + (a.Z - b.Z) ^ 2)
    End Function

    Private Function UnitXY(v As Point3d) As Point3d
        Dim magnitude As Double
        magnitude = Sqr(v.X * v.X + v.Y * v.Y)
        PLGRequire magnitude > 0#, "The geometry has a zero XY tangent (cusp or degenerate curve)."
        UnitXY = Point3dFromXYZ(v.X / magnitude, v.Y / magnitude, 0#)
    End Function

    Private Sub SameTangent(a As Point3d, b As Point3d)
        Dim u As Point3d, v As Point3d
        u = UnitXY(a)
        v = UnitXY(b)
        PLGRequire PLGPointDistance(u, v) <= ANGLE_TOL, _
            "A checked spline knot or requested component join has a corner, cusp, or discontinuous tangent. Use smooth geometry or avoid a non-tangent component join."
    End Sub

    Public Function PLGLoadPath(source As Element, ByRef reason As String) As Boolean
        Dim expected As Point3d, ending As Point3d, sourceLength As Double
        On Error GoTo Failed
        PLGClearPath
        PLGRequire source.IsTraversableElement, "This element does not expose traversable VBA geometry."
        sourceLength = GetElementPathLength(source)
        PLGRequire sourceLength > 0# And sourceLength <= 1000000000#, "Alignment length must be positive and at most 1 billion master units."
        mTolerance = 2# / ActiveModelReference.UORsPerMasterUnit
        If mTolerance < sourceLength * 0.0000000001 Then mTolerance = sourceLength * 0.0000000001
        ReDim mCurve(1 To MAX_PARTS)
        ReDim mLength(1 To MAX_PARTS)
        ReDim mBackwards(1 To MAX_PARTS)
        expected = source.AsTraversableElement.PointAtDistance(0#)
        ending = source.AsTraversableElement.PointAtDistance(sourceLength)
        mPlaneZ = expected.Z
        mPlaneTolerance = Abs(mPlaneZ) * 0.00000000000001
        If mPlaneTolerance < 0.000000000001 Then mPlaneTolerance = 0.000000000001
        PLGRequire PLGPointDistance(expected, ending) > mTolerance, "Closed alignments are not supported; use an open path."
        AppendElement source, expected, 0
        PLGRequire mCount > 0, "No supported geometry components were found."
        PLGRequire PLGPointDistance(expected, ending) <= mTolerance, "Component order does not match the selected element's direction."
        PLGRequire Abs(mTotal - sourceLength) <= mTolerance, "Component lengths disagree with the selected geometry; civil/approximated geometry is not supported."
        PLGRequire mTotal > mTolerance, "Alignment is shorter than the model distance tolerance."
        reason = "Supported VBA path: " & CStr(mCount) & " component(s), length " & Format(mTotal, "0.00")
        PLGLoadPath = True
        Exit Function
    Failed:
        reason = Err.Description & vbCrLf & _
            "Native ORD spirals/custom civil elements require a Civil SDK evaluator. No lines were created."
        PLGClearPath
    End Function

    Private Function GetElementPathLength(source As Element) As Double
        Select Case source.Type
            Case msdElementTypeLine, msdElementTypeLineString
                GetElementPathLength = source.AsLineElement.Length
            Case msdElementTypeArc
                GetElementPathLength = source.AsArcElement.Length
            Case msdElementTypeBsplineCurve
                GetElementPathLength = source.AsBsplineCurveElement.Length
            Case msdElementTypeComplexString
                GetElementPathLength = source.AsComplexStringElement.Length
            Case Else
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2602, , "Unsupported element type " & CStr(source.Type) & ". Its native geometry cannot be evaluated by this tool."
        End Select
    End Function

    Private Sub AppendElement(source As Element, expected As Point3d, ByVal depth As Long)
        Dim ee As ElementEnumerator, child As Element, vertices() As Point3d
        Dim i As Long, first As Long, last As Long, stepIndex As Long
        Dim piece As LineElement, curve As BsplineCurve
        PLGRequire depth <= 16, "Complex geometry nesting exceeds 16 levels."
        Select Case source.Type
            Case msdElementTypeComplexString
                Set ee = source.AsComplexStringElement.GetSubElements
                Do While ee.MoveNext
                    Set child = ee.Current
                    AppendElement child, expected, depth + 1
                Loop
            Case msdElementTypeLineString
                vertices = source.AsVertexList.GetVertices
                first = LBound(vertices): last = UBound(vertices): stepIndex = 1
    # ORD 2026 repeatable acceptance tests

    **Status: not executed inside ORD during implementation.** Static signature and
    source checks do not substitute for these tests. Compile and run in a disposable
    DGN/project first. Record ORD's full build, model dimensionality, working-unit
    definition, UORs per master unit, tolerance, and measured results. Do not promote
    native spiral support merely because output looks perpendicular on screen.

    ## Preparation and evidence

    1. Assemble/import as described in [README.md](README.md), then use **Debug >
       Compile**. Record compiler success/failure. Run the optional
       `modPerpendicularTests.RunSelfTests` with the generator/picker inactive. It creates
       only in-memory fixtures, verifies analytical lines/arcs/B-splines, cumulative
       joins, both directions, placements, counts, and rejection cases. It does not
       exercise AddElement, form layout, selection, or the native civil schema.
    2. Use a scratch design model with global XY plan geometry. Choose an existing
       writable level with distinctive color/style/weight; record active settings.
       Repeat with explicit symbology and ByLevel settings. Record original alignment
       IDs, lengths, and control geometry before and after each test.
    3. Record the output IDs/count. Confirm each output is an ordinary independent
       two-point line, in the active model and on the captured active level. Inspect
       color, style and weight (including preservation of ByLevel values).
    4. For each placement, obtain the actual alignment anchor (centered midpoint or
       one-sided endpoint). Compare XY with an analytical reference or an independent
       native ORD distance/tangent report. Check path distance from the direction's
       origin, not Euclidean distance between anchors.
    5. For line direction vector `v = endpoint2 - endpoint1` and reference unit tangent
       `t`, verify `abs(v.x*t.x + v.y*t.y)/|v| <= 1e-6`. Check total length to distance
       tolerance `T = max(2/UORsPerMasterUnit, pathLength*1e-10)` and coordinate/path
       residuals to `T` (or explicitly record stricter project acceptance criteria).
       `T` is the macro's numerical budget, not an agency design standard. Do not
       compare against the macro's tangent as the sole independent reference.
    6. Test with no selection, exactly one preselection, multiple preselections, an invalid
       selection, graphical locate/accept, rejection of an unsupported candidate,
       Reset cancellation, and reference attachments. Check Cancel, Escape, and X
       produce no elements; reopening retains settings while refreshing context.
       The new workflow always prompts for alignment selection first, followed by
       start and end picking. Preselection does not bypass the alignment prompt.
    7. Record actual form layout at your display scaling: no clipped rows/messages,
       keyboard tab order, Enter, Escape, numeric locale, read-only displays, live
       estimate and invalid input messages. All controls are created at runtime.

    ## 1. Straight 1,000-foot alignment

    Use a feet-master model and a horizontal line `(0,0,0)` to `(1000,0,0)`. With
    spacing 150, total length 200, centered, start 0, end-of-alignment, starting line
    included and ending line excluded:

    - Expect **7** lines at X = `0,150,300,450,600,750,900`.
    - Each line has Y endpoints `+100,-100` and Z = 0, and is exactly 200 feet long
      within `T`. Reference tangent is `(1,0)`.
    - Include Ending: **8**, with a final line at X = 1000 (last interval 100).
    - End = 900: ending excluded gives **6** at 0 through 750; including it gives **7**,
      never two coincident ending lines. Exclude both endpoints: **5**, 150 through 750.
    - Start = 50, end = 700: regular distances `50,200,350,500,650`; including the end
      adds 700. Excluding the starting line removes only 50.

    ## 2. Circular curve, known radius

    Create a counterclockwise quarter circle with center `(0,0)`, radius 500 feet,
    start `(500,0)`, end `(0,500)`, all at Z = 0. The path length is
    `500*pi/2 = 785.398163397448...` feet. Default spacing gives **6** lines at
    `0,150,300,450,600,750`; including the end gives **7**.

    At distance `s`, independent references are:

    ```text
    theta = s / 500
    p = (500*cos(theta), 500*sin(theta))
    t = (-sin(theta), cos(theta))
    ```

    At s = 150, theta = 0.3 radians. A centered 200-foot cross-line is radial, with
    endpoint radii 400 and 600 feet. Anchor-to-anchor straight distances are shorter
    than 150; **do not** treat that as a spacing failure. Repeat clockwise and through
    an arc's internal rational B-spline span boundary to exercise knot checks.

    ## 3. Clothoid spiral transition: native support gate

    Create an actual ORD clothoid with origin `(0,0)`, initial heading 0, length 300
    feet and curvature increasing from 0 to `1/1000 ft^-1` (left turn). This is a
    **native civil test**, not the cubic B-spline fixture in the self-test module.

    The mathematical reference is `theta(s) = s^2 / 600000`, with
    `x = integral(cos(theta(s)), ds)` and `y = integral(sin(theta(s)), ds)`.
    The following independent values were computed from the convergent Fresnel
    power series using 55-digit decimal arithmetic (displayed rounded):

    | s (ft) | x (ft) | y (ft) | tangent heading (rad) |
    | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
    | 0 | 0.000000000000 | 0.000000000000 | 0.000000000000 |
    | 75 | 74.999340822995 | 0.234373528621 | 0.009375000000 |
    | 150 | 149.978907623246 | 1.874811671373 | 0.037500000000 |
    | 225 | 224.839872121185 | 6.324907813699 | 0.084375000000 |
    | 300 | 299.325702760028 | 14.975910108887 | 0.150000000000 |

    Select the native element and record its VBA type/components. An opaque or
    unsupported component **must be rejected with no writes**. If ORD exposes only
    a B-spline or line-string representation, record that fact explicitly: the tool
    evaluates that representation, not the civil clothoid definition. A successful
    run alone **does not pass native spiral support**. Compare distance, point and
    tangent against the table and native ORD reports before any engineering use.
    If the representation disagrees, do not use it and do not lower tolerances; the
    native Civil SDK implementation remains required. Native spiral traversal is
    not implemented by this release.

    For an accepted, independently verified spline representation, use spacing 75
    and Include Ending to compare all five points. Check the first and last tangent,
    full path length, centered length and left/right orientation. Do not count the
    generic B-spline self-test as evidence of native spiral fidelity.

    ## 4. Tangent–spiral–curve–spiral–tangent

    Build a native continuous alignment in feet:

    | Part | Length | Curvature / heading |
    | --- | ---: | --- |
    | Initial tangent | 1000 | Heading 0 |
    | Entry clothoid | 300 | Curvature 0 to +1/1000; heading 0 to 0.15 rad |
    | Circular arc | 600 | R = 1000; heading 0.15 to 0.75 rad |
    | Exit clothoid | 300 | Curvature +1/1000 to 0; heading 0.75 to 0.9 rad |
    | Final tangent | 1000 | Heading 0.9 rad |

    Distances: TS = 1000, SC = 1300, CS = 1900, ST = 2200, end = 3200. A native
    evaluator would give **22** regular lines at `0,150,...,3150`, or **23** with the
    end included. Spacing **must not reset** at the four joins. Apply the same native
    support gate as test 3; rejection is the expected safe result when native spiral
    geometry is inaccessible. Do not claim this native test passed from display
    geometry alone.

    For independently verified exposed geometry, compare positions/tangents just
    before, at, and just after each join (e.g. `join +/- 0.001 ft`). Force exact join
    placements using start = 1000, spacing = 300 and include start. For reference:
    SC is `(1000 + 299.325702760028, 14.975910108887)`. The circle displacement is
    `1000*(sin(0.75)-sin(0.15), cos(0.15)-cos(0.75))`. The exit spiral displacement
    is the entry displacement `(299.325702760028, -14.975910108887)` rotated by
    0.9 radians. These definitions permit an independent composite reference.

    Also run a fully supported ordinary tangent–arc–tangent complex string to check
    cumulative transitions without a native spiral. The in-memory self-test checks
    the first tangent/arc join at distances 999.999, 1000 and 1000.001, and at 1050.

    ## 5. Reversed direction

    Reverse the stored direction of the straight fixture using ORD on a **copy**.
    Forward default anchors should now run X = `1000,850,...,100`. Left should be
    south and right north. Test Reverse in the dialog on the original eastbound
    fixture: the same positions/sides should result, without changing its stored
    direction. Reverse on the reversed copy restores the original direction.
    Repeat on the arc and supported complex string; compare `p(L-s)` and `-t(L-s)`.

    ## 6. Placement, level, symbology, Z and Undo

    - Eastbound straight: centered endpoints Y = +100/-100; left-only Y = +200/0;
      right-only Y = 0/-200. All lengths are 200. Reverse swaps physical sides.
    - Use a horizontal element at Z = 25 in a 3D model. Output endpoints must keep
      Z = 25. Reject a sloping 3D line; do not replace its 3D path length with XY length.
    - Repeat with a different active level/color/style/weight between runs. Check
      Generate uses the current settings. For a precise timing check, break at the
      beginning of `PLGGenerate` in a scratch project, change the active settings in
      the VBA Immediate window, then continue; the captured values must be used.
    - No new levels, level edits, source changes, or non-line output elements.
    - Use ordinary Undo until the run's additions disappear. Record the number of
      steps; a one-step group is not promised. The original geometry must remain.

    ## 7. Non-foot master units

    In a meter-master model create a 1000-meter line and enter 150/200. Expect seven
    lines separated by 150 **meters**, each 200 meters long. Repeat in millimeters
    with suitably sized geometry. No automatic foot conversion is expected. Check
    the read-only master/subunit display. Repeat international-foot and US survey-foot
    models; verify interpretation from the actual unit definition, not the short
    unit label. Use local decimal notation and test invalid unit/station suffixes.

    ## 8. Invalid inputs, unsupported geometry and write errors

    Try spacing/length 0, negative, blank, text, out-of-range numbers and values below
    four `T`; start < 0, start >= end, end beyond length, and a >10,000-line request.
    Try a range shorter than spacing with both endpoints excluded. Expect a clear
    error and zero elements, including when errors occur on a later placement.

    Test shapes, closed line strings, cells, references, opaque civil components,
    legacy type-11 curves, gaps, zero-length components, changing Z, and a line
    string with a sharp corner exactly at a requested placement. Reject these
    cases as documented. The same corner need not block placements strictly inside
    its straight segments. Reject discontinuous/cusped spline knots. Test both
    read-only models, sheet models, and locked/read-only levels: no configuration/access changes.

    For rollback, use a separate disposable project copied from this source. Insert
    a temporary `If i = 2 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2999, , "Test write failure"`
    inside the write loop **before** `AddElement`. Generate at least two lines.
    Expect the first line removed and the source intact; remove that test injection
    and recompile afterward. This tests removal after one successful write. If a
    real cleanup failure occurs, verify the message identifies it; record it as a
    failure requiring manual inspection/Undo. No filesystem or DGN transaction is
    claimed, and disk failure cannot be ruled out by this injected test.

    ## 9. Exact endpoints and session state

    Check zero and exact geometry end on straight, arc and supported spline/complex
    fixtures, on/off-grid ends, forward/reverse, with all four checkbox combinations.
    No duplicate ending line may occur. Check nonzero starts anchor their own grid.
    Verify a new alignment/model refreshes displays while retaining settings, and
    that an old numeric end outside the new path gives an error. Unload/reload the
    project or reset VBA: defaults should return. Cancel and invalid input must leave
    both the original alignment and the element count unchanged.

    ## 10. Dynamic range workflow (new; pending ORD execution)

    1. Run Main with zero, one, and multiple preselected elements. Each run must
       first prompt to identify/accept the alignment, then **START**, then **END**,
       then show settings. No DGN elements should be added during these steps.
    2. On the 1000-foot eastbound line, pick near `(100,40)` and `(700,-40)` in plan.
       The anchors must be `(100,0)` and `(700,0)`, and the fields must read 100 and
       700 master units. With spacing 150, Include Start true and Include End false,
       expect **4** output lines at 100,250,400,550 after Generate. Include End true
       should produce **5**, adding 700 exactly once.
    3. During START, confirm the moving cross-line matches total length and placement.
       During END, confirm the fixed start and moving end both display. Move through
       arc/line/spline transitions: the status distances must remain cumulative, and
       the moving line must remain perpendicular to the actual local tangent.
    4. Test a cursor with a different Z in a 3D model: the closest **XY** position
       should be unchanged. The in-memory tests now check straight, circular,
       tangent/arc composite, reversed and cubic B-spline nearest-point references.
       Check past-end picks clamp to actual geometry endpoints.
    5. Reset during the initial START: open settings with prior numeric values
       unchanged. Reset during END: keep the accepted start and leave the prior end
       unchanged. Reset during a re-pick: leave that value unchanged. Cancel or X
       afterward must create no elements. Full Alignment must set 0 / End of Alignment.
    6. Change Direction to Reverse in settings, then use Pick Start and Pick End.
       On the same line, physical X=900 to X=300 must store distances 100 to 700.
       Left/right must follow reverse travel. Changing Direction after picking
       reinterprets numeric distances; re-pick to establish particular physical limits.
    7. Try an END before/equal to START: retain the command, explain the ordering
       requirement, and add no elements. Reset must still open settings so Direction
       can be changed. Try a corner/cusp, wrong model, unsupported source and invalid
       preview length; verify useful errors and recovery without output.
    8. On a complex/line-string crossing, click exactly where distinct path distances
       share the closest XY point: reject the ambiguous pick. A normal shared join
       with one path distance must remain pickable. Multiple equally close minima
       inside a **single** B-spline remain an API limitation; use typed distances.
    9. Switch to another ORD command while picking: no modal form should reopen over
       it and no elements should appear. Re-running Main must start a fresh alignment
       selection. Repeat after changing active model. Test view pan/zoom suspension
       and resumption separately; record actual behavior in your ORD build.
    10. Confirm only Generate writes. The chosen limits and preview must not create
        levels, alter the alignment, or add transient geometry to the persistent DGN.
        Compare performance on a larger complex string; all components are searched
        each mouse move, so high-complexity paths may be slow. Verify the actual
        Dynamics drawing/erase behavior in ORD 2026, not just the callback signatures.

    ## Test record

    | Test | ORD full build / model / units | Measured results / evidence | Pass, fail, or unsupported |
    | --- | --- | --- | --- |
    | Compile + in-memory self-tests | Pending | Not run in ORD | Pending |
    | 1 straight | Pending | | Pending |
    | 2 circle | Pending | | Pending |
    | 3 native clothoid gate | Pending | Native evaluator not shipped | Unsupported unless independently established |
    | 4 native composite gate | Pending | Native evaluator not shipped | Unsupported unless independently established |
    | 5 reversed | Pending | | Pending |

    | 8 errors/rollback | Pending | | Pending |

    | 10 dynamic selection/range | Pending | User reported earlier generator working; new picking/dynamics not run by agent | Pending |

    Option Explicit

    ' Paste into a NEW blank UserForm named frmPerpendicularLines.
    ' Controls are created here; no FRX blob or VBA-project automation is needed.
    Private WithEvents txtSpacing As MSForms.TextBox
    Private WithEvents txtLength As MSForms.TextBox
    Private WithEvents txtStart As MSForms.TextBox
    Private WithEvents txtEnd As MSForms.TextBox
    Private WithEvents cboPlacement As MSForms.ComboBox
    Private WithEvents cboDirection As MSForms.ComboBox
    Private WithEvents chkStart As MSForms.CheckBox
    Private WithEvents chkEnd As MSForms.CheckBox
    Private WithEvents cmdGenerate As MSForms.CommandButton
    Private WithEvents cmdCancel As MSForms.CommandButton
    Private WithEvents cmdPickStart As MSForms.CommandButton
    Private WithEvents cmdPickEnd As MSForms.CommandButton
    Private WithEvents cmdFullAlignment As MSForms.CommandButton
    Private lblLevel As MSForms.Label
    Private lblUnits As MSForms.Label
    Private lblStatus As MSForms.Label
    Private lblEstimate As MSForms.Label
    Private mInitializing As Boolean
    Private mRequestedPick As Long

    Private Function AddLabel(ByVal controlName As String, ByVal caption As String, _
            ByVal x As Single, ByVal y As Single, ByVal width As Single, ByVal height As Single) As MSForms.Label
        Dim label As MSForms.Label
        Set label = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.Label.1", controlName, True)
        label.caption = Replace(caption, "&", "")
        If InStr(caption, "&") > 0 Then label.Accelerator = Mid$(caption, InStr(caption, "&") + 1, 1)
        label.Left = x: label.Top = y: label.width = width: label.height = height
        Set AddLabel = label
    End Function

    Private Function AddText(ByVal controlName As String, ByVal caption As String, _
            ByVal y As Single, ByVal value As String) As MSForms.TextBox
        Dim label As MSForms.Label, box As MSForms.TextBox
        Set label = AddLabel("lbl" & controlName, caption, 18, y + 3, 220, 18)
        Set box = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.TextBox.1", controlName, True)
        box.Left = 250: box.Top = y: box.width = 230: box.height = 22
        box.value = value
        Set AddText = box
    End Function

    Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()
        Dim label As MSForms.Label
        mInitializing = True
        Me.caption = "Perpendicular Line Generator"
        Me.width = 512: Me.height = 540
        Me.StartUpPosition = 1
        Me.Font.Name = "Segoe UI": Me.Font.Size = 10
        Set label = AddLabel("lblHeading", "PERPENDICULAR LINE GENERATOR", 18, 14, 462, 22)
        label.Font.Bold = True: label.Font.Size = 12
        Set label = AddLabel("lblDistances", "All distances use model MASTER units, measured along geometry from the chosen direction's beginning. These are NOT displayed ORD stations.", 18, 42, 462, 38)
        Set lblStatus = AddLabel("lblStatus", "Alignment: not selected", 18, 84, 462, 32)
        Set lblLevel = AddLabel("lblLevel", "Active level:", 18, 118, 462, 18)
        Set lblUnits = AddLabel("lblUnits", "Working units:", 18, 138, 462, 18)
        Set txtSpacing = AddText("txtSpacing", "Line &Spacing", 168, Format$(150#, "0.00"))
        Set txtLength = AddText("txtLength", "Total Line &Length", 198, Format$(200#, "0.00"))
        Set label = AddLabel("lblPlacement", "&Placement", 18, 231, 220, 18)
        Set cboPlacement = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.ComboBox.1", "cboPlacement", True)
        With cboPlacement
            .Left = 250: .Top = 228: .width = 230: .height = 22
            .Style = fmStyleDropDownList
            .AddItem "Centered on Alignment": .AddItem "Left of Alignment": .AddItem "Right of Alignment"
            .ListIndex = 0
        End With
        Set txtStart = AddText("txtStart", "&Start Distance Along Alignment", 258, Format$(0#, "0.00"))
        Set txtEnd = AddText("txtEnd", "&End Distance", 288, "End of Alignment")
        txtStart.width = 150: txtEnd.width = 150
        Set cmdPickStart = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.CommandButton.1", "cmdPickStart", True)
        With cmdPickStart
            .caption = "Pick Start": .Left = 410: .Top = 258: .width = 70: .height = 22
        End With
        Set cmdPickEnd = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.CommandButton.1", "cmdPickEnd", True)
        With cmdPickEnd
            .caption = "Pick End": .Left = 410: .Top = 288: .width = 70: .height = 22
        End With
        Set label = AddLabel("lblDirection", "&Direction", 18, 321, 220, 18)
        Set cboDirection = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.ComboBox.1", "cboDirection", True)

            .Style = fmStyleDropDownList
            .AddItem "Forward": .AddItem "Reverse": .ListIndex = 0

        Set chkStart = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.CheckBox.1", "chkStart", True)
        With chkStart
            .caption = "Include Starting Line": .Left = 18: .Top = 356: .width = 220: .height = 22: .value = True
            .Accelerator = "I"
        End With
        Set chkEnd = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.CheckBox.1", "chkEnd", True)
        With chkEnd
            .caption = "Include Ending Line": .Left = 250: .Top = 356: .width = 230: .height = 22: .value = False
            .Accelerator = "N"
        End With
        Set lblEstimate = AddLabel("lblEstimate", "Estimated lines:", 18, 390, 462, 34)
        Set label = AddLabel("lblLimit", "VBA geometry only. Native civil spirals/station equations are not interpreted. Left/right follow the chosen direction. Limit: 10,000 lines.", 18, 428, 462, 36)
        Set cmdGenerate = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.CommandButton.1", "cmdGenerate", True)
        With cmdGenerate
            .caption = "Generate Lines": .Accelerator = "G"
            .Left = 250: .Top = 476: .width = 140: .height = 28: .Default = True
        End With
        Set cmdCancel = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.CommandButton.1", "cmdCancel", True)
        With cmdCancel
            .caption = "Cancel": .Left = 400: .Top = 476: .width = 80: .height = 28: .Cancel = True

        Set cmdFullAlignment = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.CommandButton.1", "cmdFullAlignment", True)
        With cmdFullAlignment
            .caption = "Full Alignment": .Accelerator = "F"
            .Left = 18: .Top = 476: .width = 130: .height = 28
        End With
        mInitializing = False
    End Sub

    Public Sub PrepareDialog(ByVal status As String)
        mRequestedPick = 0
        RefreshContext status
    End Sub

    Public Property Get RequestedPick() As Long
        RequestedPick = mRequestedPick
    End Property

    Public Sub ReadPickSettings(ByVal target As Long, ByRef reverse As Boolean, ByRef previewLength As Double, _
            ByRef placement As Long, ByRef firstDistance As Double)
        previewLength = ReadNumber(txtLength, "Total Line Length")
        PLGRequire previewLength > 4# * PLGDistanceTolerance(), "Total Line Length must be positive and exceed four distance tolerances."
        placement = cboPlacement.ListIndex
        PLGRequire placement >= 0 And cboDirection.ListIndex >= 0, "Choose placement and direction."
        reverse = (cboDirection.ListIndex = 1)
        If target = 2 Then
            firstDistance = ReadNumber(txtStart, "Start Distance")
            PLGRequire firstDistance >= 0# And firstDistance <= PLGPathLength(), "Start Distance is outside the geometry. Pick Start again."
        End If
    End Sub

    Public Sub SetPickedDistance(ByVal target As Long, ByVal distance As Double)
        If target = 1 Then
            txtStart.value = CStr(distance)
        ElseIf Abs(distance - PLGPathLength()) <= PLGDistanceTolerance() Then
            txtEnd.value = "End of Alignment"
        Else
            txtEnd.value = CStr(distance)
        End If
        RefreshEstimate
    End Sub

    Private Sub cmdPickStart_Click()
        mRequestedPick = 1
        Me.Hide
    End Sub

    Private Sub cmdPickEnd_Click()
        mRequestedPick = 2
        Me.Hide
    End Sub

    Private Sub cmdFullAlignment_Click()
        mInitializing = True
        txtStart.value = Format$(0#, "0.00")
        txtEnd.value = "End of Alignment"
        mInitializing = False
        RefreshEstimate
    End Sub

    Public Sub RefreshContext(ByVal status As String)
        lblStatus.caption = "Alignment: " & status
        lblLevel.caption = "Active level: " & ActiveSettings.Level.Name & " (captured again on Generate)"
        lblUnits.caption = "Working units - " & PLGUnitsDescription()
        RefreshEstimate
    End Sub

    Private Function ReadNumber(box As MSForms.TextBox, ByVal field As String) As Double
        PLGRequire Len(Trim$(box.Text)) > 0, field & " is required."
        PLGRequire IsNumeric(box.Text), field & " must be a number in master units (no station notation or unit suffix)."
        ReadNumber = CDbl(box.Text)
        PLGRequire Abs(ReadNumber) <= 1000000000#, field & " must not exceed 1 billion master units."
    End Function

    Private Function ReadSettings(first As Double, last As Double, spacing As Double, totalLength As Double, _

    Attribute VB_Name = "PerpendicularLineGeneratorMain"
    Option Explicit

    ' Key-in: VBA RUN [PerpendicularLineGenerator]PerpendicularLineGeneratorMain.Main
    Private mSource As Element
    Private mModel As ModelReference
    Private mSessionActive As Boolean
    Private mStatus As String
    Private mPicker As clsPerpendicularLocate

    Public Sub Main()
        On Error GoTo Failed
        If mSessionActive Then Exit Sub
        PLGRequire ActiveModelReference.Type <> msdModelTypeSheet, "Open the alignment's design/drawing model before running this tool. Sheet models are excluded."
        CommandState.StartLocate New clsPerpendicularLocate
        Exit Sub
    Failed:
        MsgBox Err.Description, vbExclamation, "Perpendicular Line Generator"
        CommandState.StartDefaultCommand
    End Sub

    Public Function PLGBeginAlignment(source As Element) As Boolean
        Dim reason As String
        On Error GoTo Failed
        If mSessionActive Then Exit Function
        PLGRequire ActiveModelReference.Type <> msdModelTypeSheet, "Open the alignment's design/drawing model before running this tool. Sheet models are excluded."
        PLGRequire source.ModelReference.IsActive And Not source.ModelReference.IsAttachment, _
            "Select geometry in the active model. Reference attachments are not supported."
        If Not PLGLoadPath(source, reason) Then
            MsgBox reason, vbExclamation, "Unsupported alignment"
            Exit Function
        End If
        Set mSource = source
        Set mModel = ActiveModelReference
        mSessionActive = True
        mStatus = reason
        frmPerpendicularLines.RefreshContext mStatus
        BeginRangePick 1, True
        PLGBeginAlignment = True
        Exit Function
    Failed:
        MsgBox Err.Description, vbExclamation, "Perpendicular Line Generator"
        PLGCloseSession
    End Function

    Public Sub PLGCloseSession()
        Set mPicker = Nothing
        mSessionActive = False
        Set mSource = Nothing
        Set mModel = Nothing
        PLGClearPath
    End Sub

    Public Function PLGPickContextValid() As Boolean
        On Error GoTo Done
        If Not mSessionActive Then Exit Function
        If mModel Is Nothing Then Exit Function
        PLGPickContextValid = mModel.IsActive
    Done:
    End Function

    Private Sub ShowSettingsDialog()
        Dim target As Long
        On Error GoTo Failed
        PLGRequire PLGPickContextValid(), "The active model changed. Run the tool again."
        frmPerpendicularLines.PrepareDialog mStatus
        frmPerpendicularLines.Show vbModal
        target = frmPerpendicularLines.RequestedPick
        If target > 0 Then
            BeginRangePick target, False
        Else
            PLGCloseSession
        End If
        Exit Sub
    Failed:
        MsgBox Err.Description, vbExclamation, "Perpendicular Line Generator"
        PLGCloseSession
    End Sub

    Private Sub BeginRangePick(ByVal target As Long, ByVal followWithEnd As Boolean)
        Dim reverse As Boolean, previewLength As Double, placement As Long, firstDistance As Double
        Dim errorText As String, stopError As Long
        On Error GoTo Failed
        PLGRequire PLGPickContextValid(), "The active model changed. Run the tool again."
        frmPerpendicularLines.ReadPickSettings target, reverse, previewLength, placement, firstDistance
        Set mPicker = New clsPerpendicularLocate
        mPicker.ConfigurePick target, reverse, previewLength, placement, firstDistance, followWithEnd
        CommandState.StartPrimitive mPicker
        Exit Sub
    Failed:
        errorText = Err.Description
        On Error Resume Next
        Err.Clear
        If Not mPicker Is Nothing Then mPicker.StopPick Else CommandState.StartDefaultCommand
        stopError = Err.Number
        Set mPicker = Nothing
        On Error GoTo 0
        If stopError <> 0 Then
            PLGCloseSession
            MsgBox errorText & vbCrLf & "The picker could not be stopped. Start another ORD command before retrying.", vbExclamation, "Perpendicular Line Generator"
            Exit Sub
        End If
        MsgBox errorText, vbExclamation, "Perpendicular Line Generator"
        If PLGPickContextValid() Then ShowSettingsDialog Else PLGCloseSession
    End Sub

    Public Sub PLGFinishRangePick(ByVal target As Long, ByVal accepted As Boolean, _
            ByVal distance As Double, ByVal followWithEnd As Boolean)
        On Error GoTo Failed
        Set mPicker = Nothing
        PLGRequire PLGPickContextValid(), "The active model changed. Run the tool again."
        If accepted Then frmPerpendicularLines.SetPickedDistance target, distance
        If accepted And target = 1 And followWithEnd Then
            BeginRangePick 2, False
        Else
            ShowSettingsDialog
        End If
        Exit Sub
    Failed:
        MsgBox Err.Description, vbExclamation, "Perpendicular Line Generator"
        PLGCloseSession
    End Sub

    Public Function PLGUnitsDescription() As String
        Dim master As MeasurementUnit, subUnit As MeasurementUnit
        master = ActiveModelReference.MasterUnit
        subUnit = ActiveModelReference.SubUnit
        PLGUnitsDescription = "Master: " & master.Label & " | Sub: " & subUnit.Label
    End Function

    Public Function PLGGenerate(ByVal first As Double, ByVal last As Double, ByVal spacing As Double, _
            ByVal totalLength As Double, ByVal placement As Long, ByVal reverse As Boolean, _
            ByVal includeFirst As Boolean, ByVal includeLast As Boolean) As String
        Dim level As Level, style As LineStyle, color As Long, weight As Long
        Dim lines As New Collection, line As LineElement, currentSource As Element
        Dim distances() As Double, n As Long, i As Long, attempted As Long, remaining As Long
        Dim p As Point3d, t As Point3d, a As Point3d, b As Point3d
        Dim reason As String, originalError As String
        On Error GoTo Failed
        PLGRequire Not mSource Is Nothing, "Select an alignment first."
        PLGRequire mModel.IsActive, "The active model changed. Cancel and run the macro again."
        PLGRequire Not ActiveModelReference.IsReadOnly, "The active model/DGN is read-only."
        ' Capture settings on Generate; do not reuse the values shown when the dialog opened.
        Set level = ActiveSettings.Level
        Set style = ActiveSettings.LineStyle
        color = ActiveSettings.Color
        weight = ActiveSettings.LineWeight
        PLGRequire level.ElementAccess = msdLevelElementAccessAll, _
            "The active level must allow modification/deletion so failed writes can be rolled back. Choose an unlocked writable level."
        Set currentSource = mModel.GetElementByID(mSource.ID)
        If Not PLGLoadPath(currentSource, reason) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2605, , reason
        n = PLGBuildDistances(first, last, spacing, includeFirst, includeLast, distances)
        For i = 1 To n
            PLGEvaluate distances(i), reverse, p, t
            PLGEndpoints p, t, totalLength, placement, a, b
            Set line = CreateLineElement2(Nothing, a, b)
            line.Color = color
            line.LineWeight = weight
            Set line.LineStyle = style
            PLGRequire Abs(line.Length - totalLength) <= PLGDistanceTolerance(), "A prepared line has the wrong length."
            lines.Add line
        Next i
        ' All geometry and all LineElements now exist in memory; only this loop writes.
        For i = 1 To lines.Count
            Set line = lines(i)
            attempted = i
            ActiveModelReference.AddElement line
            ' Bentley forbids assigning a Level object before AddElement.
            Set line.Level = level
            line.Rewrite
        Next i
        PLGGenerate = "Generated " & CStr(n) & " perpendicular lines at " & Format(spacing, "0.00") & _
            " master-unit spacing on level " & level.Name & "." & vbCrLf & PLGUnitsDescription()
        If includeLast Then PLGGenerate = PLGGenerate & vbCrLf & "The ending line may have a shorter final spacing."
        Exit Function
    Failed:
        originalError = Err.Description
        ' Remove only this run's elements; never issue a broad Undo command.
        On Error Resume Next
        For i = attempted To 1 Step -1
            Err.Clear
            Set line = lines(i)
            If DLongToString(line.ID) <> "0" Then mModel.RemoveElement line
            If Err.Number <> 0 Then remaining = remaining + 1
        Next i
        On Error GoTo 0
        If remaining > 0 Then
            originalError = originalError & vbCrLf & "Rollback could not remove " & CStr(remaining) & _
                " element(s). Use ordinary Undo and inspect the model before retrying."
        ElseIf attempted > 0 Then
    Attribute VB_Name = "modPerpendicularGeometry"
    Option Explicit

    ' Geometry distances and coordinates are in active-model master units.
    ' Bentley ORD 2026 MicroStationVBA.chm: BsplineCurve.FromElement,
    ' ComputeCurveLength, EvaluatePointAtDistance, EvaluatePointTangent.
    ' shortcut: evaluate exposed VBA geometry only; use a Civil SDK add-in when native spirals or station equations are required.
    Public Const PLG_MAX_LINES As Long = 10000
    Private Const MAX_PARTS As Long = 10000
    Private Const ANGLE_TOL As Double = 0.000001
    Private mCurve() As BsplineCurve
    Private mLength() As Double
    Private mBackwards() As Boolean
    Private mCount As Long
    Private mTotal As Double
    Private mTolerance As Double
    Private mPlaneZ As Double
    Private mPlaneTolerance As Double

    Public Sub PLGClearPath()
        Erase mCurve
        Erase mLength
        Erase mBackwards
        mCount = 0
        mTotal = 0#
    End Sub

    Public Function PLGPathLength() As Double
        PLGPathLength = mTotal
    End Function

    Public Function PLGDistanceTolerance() As Double
        PLGDistanceTolerance = mTolerance
    End Function

    Public Sub PLGRequire(ByVal condition As Boolean, ByVal message As String)
        If Not condition Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2601, "Perpendicular Lines", message
    End Sub

    Public Function PLGPointDistance(a As Point3d, b As Point3d) As Double
        PLGPointDistance = Sqr((a.X - b.X) ^ 2 + (a.Y - b.Y) ^ 2 + (a.Z - b.Z) ^ 2)
    End Function

    Private Function UnitXY(v As Point3d) As Point3d
        Dim magnitude As Double
        magnitude = Sqr(v.X * v.X + v.Y * v.Y)
        PLGRequire magnitude > 0#, "The geometry has a zero XY tangent (cusp or degenerate curve)."
        UnitXY = Point3dFromXYZ(v.X / magnitude, v.Y / magnitude, 0#)
    End Function

    Private Sub SameTangent(a As Point3d, b As Point3d)
        Dim u As Point3d, v As Point3d
        u = UnitXY(a)
        v = UnitXY(b)
        PLGRequire PLGPointDistance(u, v) <= ANGLE_TOL, _
            "A checked spline knot or requested component join has a corner, cusp, or discontinuous tangent. Use smooth geometry or avoid a non-tangent component join."
    End Sub

    Public Function PLGLoadPath(source As Element, ByRef reason As String) As Boolean
        Dim expected As Point3d, ending As Point3d, sourceLength As Double
        On Error GoTo Failed
        PLGClearPath
        PLGRequire source.IsTraversableElement, "This element does not expose traversable VBA geometry."
        sourceLength = GetElementPathLength(source)
        PLGRequire sourceLength > 0# And sourceLength <= 1000000000#, "Alignment length must be positive and at most 1 billion master units."
        mTolerance = 2# / ActiveModelReference.UORsPerMasterUnit
        If mTolerance < sourceLength * 0.0000000001 Then mTolerance = sourceLength * 0.0000000001
        ReDim mCurve(1 To MAX_PARTS)
        ReDim mLength(1 To MAX_PARTS)
        ReDim mBackwards(1 To MAX_PARTS)
        expected = source.AsTraversableElement.PointAtDistance(0#)
        ending = source.AsTraversableElement.PointAtDistance(sourceLength)
        mPlaneZ = expected.Z
        mPlaneTolerance = Abs(mPlaneZ) * 0.00000000000001
        If mPlaneTolerance < 0.000000000001 Then mPlaneTolerance = 0.000000000001
        PLGRequire PLGPointDistance(expected, ending) > mTolerance, "Closed alignments are not supported; use an open path."
        AppendElement source, expected, 0
        PLGRequire mCount > 0, "No supported geometry components were found."
        PLGRequire PLGPointDistance(expected, ending) <= mTolerance, "Component order does not match the selected element's direction."
        PLGRequire Abs(mTotal - sourceLength) <= mTolerance, "Component lengths disagree with the selected geometry; civil/approximated geometry is not supported."
        PLGRequire mTotal > mTolerance, "Alignment is shorter than the model distance tolerance."
        reason = "Supported VBA path: " & CStr(mCount) & " component(s), length " & Format(mTotal, "0.00")
        PLGLoadPath = True
        Exit Function
    Failed:
        reason = Err.Description & vbCrLf & _
            "Native ORD spirals/custom civil elements require a Civil SDK evaluator. No lines were created."
        PLGClearPath
    End Function

    Private Function GetElementPathLength(source As Element) As Double
        Select Case source.Type
            Case msdElementTypeLine, msdElementTypeLineString
                GetElementPathLength = source.AsLineElement.Length
            Case msdElementTypeArc
                GetElementPathLength = source.AsArcElement.Length
            Case msdElementTypeBsplineCurve
                GetElementPathLength = source.AsBsplineCurveElement.Length
            Case msdElementTypeComplexString
                GetElementPathLength = source.AsComplexStringElement.Length
            Case Else
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2602, , "Unsupported element type " & CStr(source.Type) & ". Its native geometry cannot be evaluated by this tool."
        End Select
    End Function

        "body": "This is a tile of the standard one-meter resolution digital elevation model (DEM) produced through the 3D Elevation Program (3DEP) .  The elevations in this DEM represent the topographic bare-earth surface.  USGS standard one-meter DEMs are produced exclusively from high resolution light detection and ranging (lidar) source data of one-meter or higher resolution.  One-meter DEM surfaces are seamless within collection projects, but, not necessarily seamless across projects.  The spatial reference used for tiles of the one-meter DEM  within the conterminous United States (CONUS) is Universal Transverse Mercator (UTM) in units of meters, and in conformance with the North American Datum of 1983 (NAD83).  All bare earth elevation values are in meters and are referenced to the North American Vertical Datum of 1988 (NAVD88).  Each tile is distributed in the UTM Zone in which it lies.  If a tile crosses two UTM zones, it is delivered in both zones.  The one-meter DEM is the highest resolution standard DEM offered in the 3DEP product suite.  Other 3DEP products are nationally seamless DEMs in resolutions of 1/3, 1, and 2 arc seconds.  These seamless DEMs were referred to as the National Elevation Dataset (NED) from about 2000 through 2015 at which time they became the seamless DEM layers under the 3DEP program and lost the NED branding.  Other 3DEP products include five-meter DEMs in Alaska as well as various source datasets including the lidar point cloud and interferometric synthetic aperture radar (Ifsar) digital surface models and intensity images. All 3DEP products are public domain.",
