        """Explicit model-master -> CRS-native XY -> geographic boundary contract."""
        import math

        from pyproj import CRS, Geod, Transformer, network

        network.set_network_enabled(False)
        UNIT_METERS = {"m": 1.0, "ft": 0.3048, "us-ft": 1200 / 3937}
        MAX_VERTICES = 20000


        def require(condition, message):
            if not condition:
                raise ValueError(message)


        def linear_factor(source, target):
            require(source in UNIT_METERS and target in UNIT_METERS,
                    "Units must be m, ft (international), or us-ft (US survey).")
            return UNIT_METERS[source] / UNIT_METERS[target]


        def horizontal_crs(value):
            crs = CRS.from_user_input(value)
            require(crs.is_projected and len(crs.axis_info) == 2,
                    "Provide a two-dimensional projected horizontal CRS; no compound, vertical, or geographic DGN CRS.")
            require(all(a.direction in ("east", "north") for a in crs.axis_info),
                    "Only east/north projected coordinate axes are supported.")
            factors = [a.unit_conversion_factor for a in crs.axis_info]
            require(abs(factors[0] - factors[1]) < 1e-14, "Mixed horizontal axis units are unsupported.")
            return crs


        def transformer(source, target):
            # Fail if PROJ's best operation needs missing grids; never allow ballpark substitution.
            return Transformer.from_crs(source, target, always_xy=True,
                                       allow_ballpark=False, only_best=True)


        def ring_valid(points):
            require(isinstance(points, list) and 3 <= len(points) <= MAX_VERTICES,
                    "Boundary needs 3 to 20,000 XY vertices.")
            ring = []
            for point in points:
                require(len(point) == 2, "Boundary vertices must contain XY only.")
                p = [float(v) for v in point]
                require(all(math.isfinite(v) for v in p), "Nonfinite boundary coordinate.")
                ring.append(p)
            if ring[0] != ring[-1]:
                ring.append(ring[0][:])
            require(len(ring) >= 4, "Boundary has fewer than three distinct corners.")
            require(all(a != b for a, b in zip(ring, ring[1:])), "Duplicate adjacent boundary vertices.")
            # Translate before shoelace to avoid cancellation at large State Plane coordinates.
            x0, y0 = ring[0]
            area = sum((a[0]-x0)*(b[1]-y0)-(b[0]-x0)*(a[1]-y0)
                       for a, b in zip(ring, ring[1:])) / 2
            require(abs(area) > 1e-10, "Boundary has zero XY area.")
            return ring


        def densify(ring, native_to_meters, segment_m=100):
            result = []
            for a, b in zip(ring, ring[1:]):
                n = max(1, math.ceil(math.dist(a, b) * native_to_meters / segment_m))
                require(len(result) + n < MAX_VERTICES, "Boundary too large/complex; reduce the area.")
                result.extend([[a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*i/n, a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*i/n]
                               for i in range(n)])
            result.append(result[0][:])
            return result


        def prepare_boundary(config):
            require(config.get("crs_confirmed") is True,
                    "Confirm source CRS, master-unit type, and absence of local/ground transforms.")
            require(config.get("coordinates_are_grid") is True,
                    "Local offsets, rotation, ground scale, or custom Bentley GCS modifiers require a validated adapter.")
            crs = horizontal_crs(config["source_crs"])
            unit = config.get("master_unit")
            require(unit in UNIT_METERS, "Explicit model master unit required: m, ft, or us-ft.")
            native_factor = crs.axis_info[0].unit_conversion_factor
            scale = UNIT_METERS[unit] / native_factor
            ring = ring_valid(config["boundary"])
            ring = [[x*scale, y*scale] for x, y in ring]
            dense = densify(ring, native_factor)
            operation = transformer(crs, "EPSG:4326")
            xs, ys = operation.transform(*zip(*dense), errcheck=True)
            require(all(math.isfinite(x) and math.isfinite(y) and -180 <= x <= 180 and -90 < y < 90
                        for x, y in zip(xs, ys)), "Transformed boundary is not valid longitude/latitude.")
            bbox = [min(xs)-1e-7, min(ys)-1e-7, max(xs)+1e-7, max(ys)+1e-7]
            require(bbox[2]-bbox[0] <= 2 and bbox[3]-bbox[1] <= 2,
                    "Extent exceeds the 2-degree safety limit (antimeridian areas unsupported).")
            area, _ = Geod(ellps="WGS84").polygon_area_perimeter(xs, ys)
            require(0 < abs(area) <= 1e10, "Geographic area must be positive and <= 10,000 square km.")
            bounds = crs.area_of_use
            if bounds:
                require(all(bounds.west-0.01 <= x <= bounds.east+0.01 and
                            bounds.south-0.01 <= y <= bounds.north+0.01 for x, y in zip(xs, ys)),
                        "Boundary lies outside the CRS area of use. Verify CRS and model coordinates.")
            return {"crs_wkt": crs.to_wkt(), "crs_name": crs.name,
                    "horizontal_unit": crs.axis_info[0].unit_name,
                    "model_to_native_scale": scale, "ring": dense, "bbox": bbox,
                    "area_m2": abs(area), "search_operation": operation.description,
                    "search_operation_accuracy_m": operation.accuracy,
                    "search_pipeline": operation.get_last_used_operation().definition}

        """TNM Access API and bounded, atomic HTTPS downloads; no third-party HTTP client."""
        import hashlib
        import json
        import time
        from pathlib import Path
        from urllib.error import HTTPError, URLError
        from urllib.parse import urlencode, urlparse
        from urllib.request import Request, urlopen

        from crs_utils import require

        BASE = "https://tnmaccess.nationalmap.gov/api/v1/"
        DATASETS = {
            "dem1m": "Digital Elevation Model (DEM) 1 meter",
            "dem13": "National Elevation Dataset (NED) 1/3 arc-second",
            "lidar": "Lidar Point Cloud (LPC)",
        }


        def https_url(url):
            p = urlparse(url)
            require(p.scheme == "https" and p.hostname and not p.username and not p.password,
                    "Only credential-free HTTPS URLs are allowed.")
            return url


        def open_https(url):
            response = urlopen(Request(https_url(url), headers={"User-Agent": "ORD-USGSTerrainDownloader/0.1"}), timeout=30)
            try:
                https_url(response.geturl())
            except Exception:
                response.close()
                raise
            return response


        class TNMClient:
            def __init__(self, opener=open_https, check=lambda: None, progress=lambda message: None):
                self.opener, self.check, self.progress = opener, check, progress

            def get_json(self, endpoint, params=None):
                url = BASE + endpoint + ("?" + urlencode(params) if params else "")
                for attempt in range(3):
                    self.check()
                    try:
                        with self.opener(url) as response:
                            raw = response.read(16 * 1024 * 1024 + 1)
                            require(len(raw) <= 16 * 1024 * 1024, "API response exceeds 16 MiB.")
                            return json.loads(raw)
                    except (HTTPError, URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as error:
                        if isinstance(error, HTTPError) and error.code not in (429, 500, 502, 503, 504):
                            raise
                        if attempt == 2:
                            raise
                        for _ in range(10 * (attempt + 1)):
                            self.check()
                            time.sleep(0.1)

            def search(self, kind, bbox, limit=500):
                require(kind in DATASETS, "Unknown dataset choice.")
                require(len(bbox) == 4 and -180 <= bbox[0] < bbox[2] <= 180 and
                        -90 <= bbox[1] < bbox[3] <= 90, "Invalid WGS84 bounding box.")
                require(1 <= limit <= 2000, "Search limit must be 1 to 2,000 products.")
                catalog = self.get_json("datasets")
                tags = set()
                def collect(rows):
                    require(isinstance(rows, list), "Malformed USGS dataset catalog.")
                    for row in rows:
                        tags.add(row.get("sbDatasetTag"))
                        collect(row.get("tags", []))
                collect(catalog)
                require(DATASETS[kind] in tags, "Dataset identifier missing from live TNM catalog; update required.")
                offset, products, seen_ids, seen_urls, seen_pages = 0, [], set(), set(), set()
                while True:
                    self.progress(f"Search: requesting results at offset {offset}")
                    page = self.get_json("products", {"datasets": DATASETS[kind],
                        "bbox": ",".join(format(v, ".10f") for v in bbox),
                        "prodFormats": "LAS,LAZ" if kind == "lidar" else "GeoTIFF",
                        "max": 100, "offset": offset, "outputFormat": "JSON"})
                    require(not page.get("errors"), f"USGS API errors: {page.get('errors')}")
                    rows, total = page.get("items"), page.get("total")
                    require(isinstance(rows, list) and isinstance(total, int) and total >= 0,
                            "Malformed USGS product response.")
                    require(total <= limit, f"USGS returned {total} products; limit {limit}. Draw a smaller boundary.")
                    if not rows:
                        require(offset >= total, "USGS pagination stopped before all products arrived. Retry search.")
                        break
                    signature = json.dumps(rows, sort_keys=True)
                    require(signature not in seen_pages, "USGS repeated a page; search stopped.")
                    seen_pages.add(signature)
                    for item in rows:
                        product = normalize(item, kind)
                        if product["id"] in seen_ids or product["url"] in seen_urls:
                            continue
                        seen_ids.add(product["id"])
                        seen_urls.add(product["url"])
                        b = product["bbox"]
                        if b and not (b[2] <= bbox[0] or b[0] >= bbox[2] or b[3] <= bbox[1] or b[1] >= bbox[3]):
                            products.append(product)
                    offset += len(rows)
                    require(offset <= 2000, "Pagination safety limit exceeded.")
                    if offset >= total:
                        break
                return products


        def normalize(item, kind):
            urls = item.get("urls") or {}
            url = (item.get("downloadLazURL") or urls.get("LAZ") or urls.get("LAS") or item.get("downloadURL")) if kind == "lidar" else (
                item.get("downloadURLRaster") or urls.get("TIFF") or urls.get("GeoTIFF") or item.get("downloadURL"))
            https_url(url or "")
            suffix = Path(urlparse(url).path).suffix.lower()
            require(suffix in ((".las", ".laz") if kind == "lidar" else (".tif", ".tiff")),
                    "USGS product is not a direct GeoTIFF/LAS/LAZ download; archives require manual handling.")
            b = item.get("boundingBox") or {}
            bbox = [float(b[k]) for k in ("minX", "minY", "maxX", "maxY")] if b else None
            require(bbox is not None and all(__import__('math').isfinite(v) for v in bbox)
                    and bbox[0] < bbox[2] and bbox[1] < bbox[3], "Product has missing/invalid geographic coverage.")
            size = item.get("sizeInBytes")
            require(size is None or isinstance(size, (int, float)) and size >= 0, "Invalid product size.")
            return {"id": str(item.get("sourceId") or hashlib.sha256(url.encode()).hexdigest()),
                    "title": str(item.get("title") or "Untitled product"), "url": url,
                    "bytes": int(size) if size else None, "bbox": bbox, "kind": kind,
                    "resolution": {"dem1m": "1 meter", "dem13": "1/3 arc-second", "lidar": "point cloud"}[kind],
                    "publication": item.get("publicationDate"), "updated": item.get("lastUpdated"),
                    "acquisition": item.get("acquisitionDate"), "metadata_url": item.get("metaUrl"),
                    "vertical": "Unverified: inspect source and product metadata before processing",
                    "raw": item}


        def download(product, folder, remaining_bytes, check=lambda: None, progress=lambda message: None,
                     opener=open_https):
            """Restart interrupted transfer safely; only reuse files with a matching local SHA-256 receipt."""
            require(remaining_bytes > 0, "Download budget exhausted.")
            folder = Path(folder)
            folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            suffix = Path(urlparse(https_url(product["url"])).path).suffix.lower()
            require(suffix in (".tif", ".tiff", ".las", ".laz"), "Unsupported source file type.")
            path = folder / (hashlib.sha256(product["url"].encode()).hexdigest()[:24] + suffix)
            receipt = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".json")
            if path.exists() and receipt.exists():
                saved = json.loads(receipt.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                digest = hashlib.sha256()
                with path.open("rb") as stream:
                    for block in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024*1024), b""):
                        check()
                        digest.update(block)
                if saved.get("url") == product["url"] and saved.get("sha256") == digest.hexdigest():
                    require(0 < path.stat().st_size <= remaining_bytes, "Cached source exceeds download budget.")
                    return path, saved
            require(not path.exists(), f"Existing source lacks a matching receipt: {path}. Move it aside before retrying.")
            temp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".part")
            digest, received = hashlib.sha256(), 0
            try:
                with opener(product["url"]) as response, temp.open("wb") as stream:
                    length = response.headers.get("Content-Length")
                    length = int(length) if length is not None else None
                    if length is not None:
                        require(0 < length <= remaining_bytes, "Server download size exceeds remaining budget.")
                    while True:
                        check()
                        block = response.read(1024*1024)
                        if not block:
                            break
                        received += len(block)
                        require(received <= remaining_bytes, "Actual transfer exceeds download budget.")
                        stream.write(block)
                        digest.update(block)
                        progress(f"Download: {product['title']} — {received / 1048576:.1f} MiB")
                require(received > 0 and (length is None or received == length), "Incomplete/empty HTTP download.")
                # TNM sizes are estimates; HTTP length + GDAL inspection are the integrity gates.
                saved = {"url": product["url"], "bytes": received, "sha256": digest.hexdigest(),
                         "catalog_bytes": product.get("bytes")}
                temp.replace(path)
                receipt.write_text(json.dumps(saved, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
                return path, saved
            finally:
                temp.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        PY

        """Auditable DEM processing. Horizontal PROJ pipeline never transforms Z."""
        import json
        import math
        import subprocess
        from pathlib import Path

        from pyproj import CRS
        from pyproj.crs import CompoundCRS
        from crs_utils import UNIT_METERS, horizontal_crs, linear_factor, require, transformer


        def gis():
            try:
                from osgeo import gdal, ogr, osr
                import numpy as np
            except ImportError as exc:
                raise RuntimeError("GDAL Python bindings and numpy are required. Use the configured QGIS/OSGeo4W Python environment.") from exc
            require(int(gdal.VersionInfo()) >= 3080000, "GDAL >= 3.8 is required.")
            gdal.UseExceptions()
            ogr.UseExceptions()
            return gdal, ogr, osr, np


        def unit_name(value):
            value = value.strip().lower()
            if value in ("m", "meter", "metre", "meters", "metres"):
                return "m"
            if value in ("us-ft", "us survey foot", "us survey feet", "foot_us", "ftus"):
                return "us-ft"
            if value in ("ft", "international foot", "international feet"):
                return "ft"
            require(not value, f"Ambiguous/unsupported embedded elevation unit: {value!r}. Verify outside this tool.")
            return None


        def vertical_contract(confirmation):
            require(isinstance(confirmation, dict), "Each source needs a vertical confirmation.")
            require(confirmation.get("unit") in UNIT_METERS, "Confirm source elevation unit: m, ft, or us-ft.")
            datum = CRS.from_user_input(confirmation.get("crs", ""))
            require(datum.is_vertical, "Supply an explicit vertical CRS such as EPSG:5703 (only when verified).")
            require(len(confirmation.get("evidence", "").strip()) >= 12,
                    "Document source-specific vertical datum/unit evidence (metadata URL, report, or embedded tags).")
            return datum


        def output_vertical(datum, unit):
            obj = datum.to_json_dict()
            obj.pop("id", None)
            obj["name"] = f"{datum.datum.name} height ({unit})"
            obj["coordinate_system"]["axis"][0]["unit"] = {
                "type": "LinearUnit", "name": {"m": "metre", "ft": "foot", "us-ft": "US survey foot"}[unit],
                "conversion_factor": UNIT_METERS[unit]}
            return CRS.from_json_dict(obj)


        def inspect_raster(path, confirmation):
            gdal, _, _, _ = gis()
            ds = gdal.Open(str(path), gdal.GA_ReadOnly)
            require(ds is not None and ds.RasterCount == 1, "DEM must be a readable, single-band raster.")
            require(ds.GetProjection(), f"Source raster has no CRS: {path}")
            declared = CRS.from_wkt(ds.GetProjection())
            hcrs = declared.to_2d()
            require(hcrs.is_projected or hcrs.is_geographic, "Unsupported raster horizontal CRS.")
            band = ds.GetRasterBand(1)
            scale, offset = band.GetScale(), band.GetOffset()
            require(scale in (None, 1) and offset in (None, 0), "Scaled/offset raster bands require explicit unpacking outside this tool.")
            require(not gdal.DataTypeIsComplex(band.DataType), "Complex-valued DEM is unsupported.")
            datum = vertical_contract(confirmation)
            embedded_unit = unit_name(band.GetUnitType())
            if embedded_unit:
                require(embedded_unit == confirmation["unit"], "Source elevation unit contradicts its embedded band unit.")
            for part in declared.sub_crs_list:
                if part.is_vertical:
                    require(part.datum == datum.datum, "Source vertical datum contradicts confirmed vertical datum.")
                    factor = part.axis_info[0].unit_conversion_factor
                    require(abs(factor - UNIT_METERS[confirmation["unit"]]) < 1e-12,
                            "Source vertical CRS unit contradicts confirmed elevation unit.")
            gt = ds.GetGeoTransform()
            require(gt[1] > 0 and gt[5] < 0 and gt[2] == 0 and gt[4] == 0,
                    "Only north-up unrotated source DEMs are supported.")
            info = {"path": str(path), "crs_wkt": declared.to_wkt(), "horizontal_wkt": hcrs.to_wkt(),
                    "geotransform": list(gt), "width": ds.RasterXSize, "height": ds.RasterYSize,
                    "band_type": gdal.GetDataTypeName(band.DataType),
                    "nodata": str(band.GetNoDataValue()), "band_unit": band.GetUnitType(),
                    "vertical_confirmation": confirmation, "dataset_metadata": ds.GetMetadata()}
            ds = None
            return info, hcrs, datum


        def compatible_tiles(infos, crss, confirmations, overlap_confirmed):
            first = infos[0]
            gt = first["geotransform"]
            for info, crs, confirmation in zip(infos, crss, confirmations):
                require(crs.equals(crss[0], ignore_axis_order=True),
                        "Mosaic source horizontal CRSs differ; select one compatible collection.")
                other = info["geotransform"]
                require(abs(other[1]/gt[1]-1) < 1e-9 and abs(other[5]/gt[5]-1) < 1e-9,
                        "Conflicting source resolutions; choose compatible products.")
                require(confirmation["unit"] == confirmations[0]["unit"], "Mixed source elevation units are unsupported.")
                for origin, step, coordinate in ((gt[0], gt[1], other[0]), (gt[3], gt[5], other[3])):
                    delta = (coordinate-origin)/step
                    require(abs(delta-round(delta)) < 1e-6, "Source grids are misaligned; mosaic requires aligned tiles.")
            def extent(info):
                g = info["geotransform"]
                return g[0], g[3]+g[5]*info["height"], g[0]+g[1]*info["width"], g[3]
            overlaps = []
            for i, a in enumerate(infos):
                ea = extent(a)
                for j in range(i):
                    eb = extent(infos[j])
                    if min(ea[2], eb[2])-max(ea[0], eb[0]) > gt[1]*1e-6 and min(ea[3], eb[3])-max(ea[1], eb[1]) > -gt[5]*1e-6:
                        overlaps.append([j, i])
            require(not overlaps or overlap_confirmed,
                    "Selected DEMs overlap. Review dates/collections and explicitly confirm list-order priority (last selected wins).")
            return overlaps


        def process_dem(paths, products, config, boundary, folder, check=lambda: None, progress=lambda message: None):
            gdal, ogr, osr, np = gis()
            folder = Path(folder)
            require(folder.is_dir(), "Processing folder does not exist.")
            target = horizontal_crs(boundary["crs_wkt"])
            confirmations = [config.get("vertical_confirmations", {}).get(p["id"], config.get("source_vertical")) for p in products]
            infos, crss, datums = [], [], []
            for path, confirmation in zip(paths, confirmations):
                check()
                info, crs, datum = inspect_raster(path, confirmation)
                infos.append(info); crss.append(crs); datums.append(datum)
            require(all(d.datum == datums[0].datum for d in datums),
                    "Mixed vertical datums: no validated vertical transformation is implemented.")
            overlaps = compatible_tiles(infos, crss, confirmations, config.get("overlap_order_confirmed") is True)
            output_unit = config.get("elevation_unit", "m")
            factor = linear_factor(confirmations[0]["unit"], output_unit)
            resolution = float(config["resolution"])
            require(math.isfinite(resolution) and resolution > 0, "Output resolution must be positive in target CRS native units.")
            ring = boundary["ring"]
            polygon = ogr.Geometry(ogr.wkbPolygon)
            linear = ogr.Geometry(ogr.wkbLinearRing)
            for x, y in ring:
                linear.AddPoint_2D(x, y)
            polygon.AddGeometry(linear)
            require(polygon.IsValid(), "Boundary is self-intersecting or invalid. Redraw the boundary.")
            xs, ys = zip(*ring)
            bounds = [min(xs), min(ys), max(xs), max(ys)]
            width, height = math.ceil((bounds[2]-bounds[0])/resolution), math.ceil((bounds[3]-bounds[1])/resolution)
            require(width > 0 and height > 0 and width*height <= 100_000_000,
                    "Output exceeds 100 million cells or is empty. Increase cell size or reduce boundary.")
            srs = osr.SpatialReference()
            srs.ImportFromWkt(target.to_wkt())
            srs.SetAxisMappingStrategy(osr.OAMS_TRADITIONAL_GIS_ORDER)
            cut = folder / "boundary.geojson"
            cut.write_text(json.dumps({"type": "FeatureCollection", "crs": {"type": "name", "properties": {"name": target.to_wkt()}},
                "features": [{"type": "Feature", "properties": {}, "geometry": json.loads(polygon.ExportToJson())}]}), encoding="utf-8")
            layer_ds = ogr.GetDriverByName("Memory").CreateDataSource("")
            layer = layer_ds.CreateLayer("boundary", srs, ogr.wkbPolygon)
            feature = ogr.Feature(layer.GetLayerDefn()); feature.SetGeometry(polygon); layer.CreateFeature(feature)
            feature = None
            operation = transformer(crss[0].to_2d(), target)
            # Resolve the operation with an actual point before passing the exact pipeline to GDAL.
            source_gt = infos[0]["geotransform"]
            operation.transform(source_gt[0], source_gt[3], errcheck=True)
            operation = operation.get_last_used_operation()
            vrt_path, temp, output = folder / "mosaic.vrt", folder / "terrain.partial.tif", folder / "terrain.tif"
            require(not output.exists() and not temp.exists(), "Output already exists; use a new run folder.")
            callback_error = []
            def callback(fraction, message, data):
                try:
                    check()
                    progress(f"Processing: horizontal warp {fraction:.0%}")
                    return 1
                except Exception as error:
                    callback_error.append(error)
                    return 0
            nodata = -1e300
            vrt = None
            try:
                vrt = gdal.BuildVRT(str(vrt_path), [str(p) for p in paths],
                                    options=gdal.BuildVRTOptions(resolution="highest", strict=True))
                require(vrt is not None, "GDAL could not construct the mosaic.")
                progress("Processing: mosaic and horizontal reprojection")
                try:
                    warped = gdal.Warp(str(temp), vrt, options=gdal.WarpOptions(
                        format="GTiff", dstSRS=target.to_wkt(), coordinateOperation=operation.definition,
                        outputBounds=bounds, xRes=resolution, yRes=resolution,
                        outputType=gdal.GDT_Float64, dstNodata=nodata,
                        cutlineDSName=str(cut) if config.get("clip", True) else None,
                        resampleAlg="bilinear", errorThreshold=0.0, multithread=False,
                        options=["-novshift"], callback=callback,
                        creationOptions=["TILED=YES", "COMPRESS=DEFLATE", "BIGTIFF=IF_SAFER"])))
                except RuntimeError:
                    if callback_error:
                        raise callback_error[0]
                    raise
                require(warped is not None, "GDAL warp produced no output.")
                # Rasterize expected coverage separately: NoData inside the polygon is a coverage gap.
                mask = gdal.GetDriverByName("MEM").Create("", warped.RasterXSize, warped.RasterYSize, 1, gdal.GDT_Byte)
                mask.SetGeoTransform(warped.GetGeoTransform()); mask.SetProjection(target.to_wkt())
                gdal.RasterizeLayer(mask, [1], layer, burn_values=[1])
                band = warped.GetRasterBand(1)
                valid_count = expected = gaps = 0
                minimum, maximum = math.inf, -math.inf
                for y in range(0, warped.RasterYSize, 256):
                    for x in range(0, warped.RasterXSize, 256):
                        check()
                        w, h = min(256, warped.RasterXSize-x), min(256, warped.RasterYSize-y)
                        a = band.ReadAsArray(x, y, w, h)
                        inside = mask.GetRasterBand(1).ReadAsArray(x, y, w, h) == 1
                        valid = a != nodata
                        require(np.isfinite(a[valid]).all(), "Nonfinite raster elevation detected.")
                        expected += int(inside.sum()); gaps += int((inside & ~valid).sum())
                        # Float64 conversion applies only to valid samples, independently of XY warp.
                        a[valid] *= factor
                        require(np.isfinite(a[valid]).all() and not np.any(a[valid] == nodata), "Elevation conversion overflow/NoData collision.")
                        samples = a[valid & inside]
                        if samples.size:
                            minimum = min(minimum, float(samples.min())); maximum = max(maximum, float(samples.max()))
                            valid_count += int(samples.size)
                        band.WriteArray(a, x, y)
                    progress(f"Processing: elevation units / coverage {min(y+256, warped.RasterYSize)}/{warped.RasterYSize} rows")
                require(expected > 0 and valid_count > 0, "No valid DEM cell centers inside boundary. Reduce cell size or verify coverage.")
                require(gaps == 0 or config.get("allow_gaps") is True,
                        f"Coverage gap: {gaps}/{expected} boundary cells are NoData. Select more coverage; output not released.")
                vertical = output_vertical(datums[0], output_unit)
                compound = CompoundCRS("DGN horizontal + verified source vertical (no datum shift)", [target, vertical])
                warped.SetProjection(compound.to_wkt())
                band.SetUnitType(output_unit)
                warped.SetMetadataItem("VERTICAL_DATUM", datums[0].datum.name)
                warped.SetMetadataItem("ELEVATION_UNIT", output_unit)
                warped.SetMetadataItem("VERTICAL_OPERATION", "Unit scaling only; no vertical datum transformation")
                warped.SetMetadataItem("SOURCE_REPORT", "report.json")
                band.FlushCache(); warped.FlushCache(); band = None; warped = None; mask = None; vrt = None
                verify = gdal.Open(str(temp))
                require(verify is not None and CRS.from_wkt(verify.GetProjection()).equals(compound), "Output CRS verification failed.")
                require(verify.GetRasterBand(1).GetUnitType() == output_unit, "Output elevation unit tag verification failed.")
                require(verify.GetRasterBand(1).Checksum() >= 0, "Output read-back checksum failed.")
                geotransform = list(verify.GetGeoTransform())
                verify = None
                check()
                temp.replace(output)
                return {"output": str(output), "sources": infos, "output_crs_wkt": compound.to_wkt(),
                        "horizontal_pipeline": operation.definition, "horizontal_accuracy_m": operation.accuracy,
                        "vertical_datum": datums[0].datum.name, "vertical_unit": output_unit,
                        "elevation_multiplier": factor, "vertical_transformation": "none; unit conversion only",
                        "resolution": resolution, "resampling": "bilinear", "geotransform": geotransform,
                        "valid_boundary_cells": valid_count, "expected_boundary_cells": expected,
                        "gap_cells": gaps, "overlaps": overlaps, "overlap_policy": "selected list order, last wins",
                        "elevation_range": [minimum, maximum], "gdal_version": gdal.VersionInfo("--version"),
                        "warning": "Not survey-grade. No vertical datum shift performed. Validate with project control before design use."}
            finally:
                vrt = None
                # A failed/cancelled raster is never named terrain.tif.
                temp.unlink(missing_ok=True)


        def inspect_lidar(path, executable, check=lambda: None):
            """Optional PDAL metadata/schema only. No ground classification or rasterization implied."""
            require(Path(executable).is_file(), "Configure a full PDAL executable path for point-cloud inspection.")
            check()
            result = subprocess.run([str(executable), "info", "--metadata", "--schema", str(path)],
                                    capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=180, check=True)
            check()
            return json.loads(result.stdout)

        """One request per subprocess. Atomic JSON plus UTF-16 UI files; cancellation is cooperative."""
        import hashlib
        import json
        import os
        import shutil
        import sys
        import time
        import traceback
        from datetime import datetime, timezone
        from pathlib import Path

        from crs_utils import prepare_boundary, require
        from usgs_client import TNMClient, download


        def atomic_json(path, value):
            temp = Path(str(path) + ".tmp")
            temp.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=True, allow_nan=False), encoding="utf-8")
            temp.replace(path)


        def clean(value):
            return str(value if value is not None else "unknown").replace("\t", " ").replace("\r", " ").replace("\n", " ")


        def ui_file(path, lines):
            temp = Path(str(path) + ".tmp")
            temp.write_text("\r\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-16")
            # An ORD reader may briefly hold the old file on Windows.
            for attempt in range(20):
                try:
                    temp.replace(path)
                    return
                except PermissionError:
                    if attempt == 19:
                        raise
                    time.sleep(0.025)


        def fingerprint(boundary):
            return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps({k: boundary[k] for k in ("crs_wkt", "ring", "model_to_native_scale")},
                                            sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()


        class Job:
            def __init__(self, folder):
                self.folder = Path(folder)
                self.last = 0
                self.state = "starting"

            def check(self):
                if (self.folder / "cancel.flag").exists():
                    raise InterruptedError("Cancelled; original completed downloads retained. No partial raster released.")

            def status(self, message, force=False):
                if force or time.monotonic()-self.last > 0.2:
                    self.last = time.monotonic()
                    atomic_json(self.folder / "status.json", {"state": self.state, "message": message})
                    ui_file(self.folder / "status.txt", [self.state, clean(message)])


        def run(config, job):
            require(config.get("schema_version") == 1, "Unsupported request schema version.")
            require(config.get("action") in ("search", "process"), "Action must be search or process.")
            job.check()
            boundary = prepare_boundary(config)
            if config["action"] == "search":
                job.state = "searching"
                job.status("Validating boundary / TNM catalog", True)
                client = TNMClient(check=job.check, progress=job.status)
                products = client.search(config["dataset"], boundary["bbox"])
                manifest = {"schema_version": 1, "utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                            "boundary": boundary, "boundary_fingerprint": fingerprint(boundary),
                            "dataset": config["dataset"], "products": products}
                atomic_json(job.folder / "search.json", manifest)
                ui_file(job.folder / "products.tsv", ["\t".join(clean(v) for v in (
                    p["id"], p["title"], p["bytes"], p["publication"], p["resolution"], p["bbox"],
                    p["vertical"], p["metadata_url"], json.dumps(p["raw"], ensure_ascii=True))) for p in products])
                ui_file(job.folder / "extent.txt", [f"WGS84 lon/lat bbox: {boundary['bbox']}",
                    f"{boundary['crs_name']} | {boundary['horizontal_unit']} | {boundary['area_m2']/1e6:.3f} km2"])
                job.state = "search_complete"
                job.status(f"{len(products)} overlapping candidates. Review metadata and select products; search bbox may overselect irregular boundaries.", True)
                return manifest
            manifest_path = Path(config["search_manifest"])
            require(manifest_path.is_file() and manifest_path.stat().st_size < 32*1024*1024, "Missing/oversized search manifest.")
            manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            require(manifest["boundary_fingerprint"] == fingerprint(boundary), "Boundary/CRS changed since search. Search again.")
            require(manifest["dataset"] == config["dataset"], "Dataset changed since search. Search again.")
            ids = config.get("selected_ids")
            require(isinstance(ids, list) and len(ids) > 0 and len(ids) == len(set(ids)), "Select unique product IDs.")
            selected = [p for p in manifest["products"] if p["id"] in ids]
            require(len(selected) == len(ids), "Selected IDs are absent from the search results.")
            budget = config.get("max_download_bytes", 5*1024**3)
            require(isinstance(budget, int) and 0 < budget <= 100*1024**3, "Download cap must be > 0 and <= 100 GiB.")
            known = sum(p["bytes"] or 0 for p in selected)
            require(known <= budget, "Selected catalog estimate exceeds download cap.")
            require(all(p["bytes"] for p in selected) or config.get("unknown_size_confirmed") is True,
                    "A selected product has unknown size. Explicit confirmation is required; hard transfer cap still applies.")
            require(shutil.disk_usage(job.folder).free > max(known, budget if not known else known)*2 + 256*1024**2,
                    "Insufficient free space for sources and processing. Choose a different destination or reduce selection/cap.")
            if config["dataset"] != "lidar":
                # Validate vertical declarations before potentially large transfers.
                from terrain_processor import gis, vertical_contract
                gis()
                for p in selected:
                    vertical_contract(config.get("vertical_confirmations", {}).get(p["id"], config.get("source_vertical")))
            report = {"schema_version": 1, "utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), "state": "downloading",
                      "request": config, "search_manifest": str(manifest_path.resolve()), "boundary": boundary,
                      "products": selected, "downloads": [], "sources_preserved": True,
                      "limits": "No survey-grade accuracy claimed. Vertical datum transformations are not implemented."}
            atomic_json(job.folder / "report.json", report)
            try:
                job.state = "downloading"
                paths, used = [], 0
                # Sources live in the destination cache, outside individual job folders; receipts enable safe retry.
                sources = job.folder.parent / "originals"
                for p in selected:
                    job.check()
                    job.status(f"Download: {p['title']}", True)
                    path, receipt = download(p, sources, budget-used, job.check, job.status)
                    used += receipt["bytes"]; paths.append(path)
                    report["downloads"].append({"product_id": p["id"], "path": str(path), **receipt})
                    atomic_json(job.folder / "report.json", report)
                if config["dataset"] == "lidar":
                    report["lidar_inspection"] = []
                    if config.get("pdal_executable"):
                        from terrain_processor import inspect_lidar
                        job.state = "inspecting_lidar"
                        for path in paths:
                            report["lidar_inspection"].append(inspect_lidar(path, config["pdal_executable"], job.check))
                        report["state"] = "lidar_downloaded_inspected"
                    else:
                        report["state"] = "lidar_downloaded_uninspected"
                    report["warning"] = "Original LAS/LAZ only; no ground classification, bare-earth raster, or terrain model created. Review point CRS, vertical metadata, and classification schema with PDAL/QGIS."
                else:
                    from terrain_processor import process_dem
                    job.state = "processing"
                    report["processing"] = process_dem(paths, selected, config, boundary, job.folder, job.check, job.status)
                    report["state"] = "dem_complete"
                    (job.folder / "ORD_IMPORT.txt").write_text(
                        "Import terrain.tif manually in a disposable ORD design model first.\n"
                        "Review report.json: horizontal CRS/native units, vertical datum, separate elevation unit, gaps and overlaps.\n"
                        "GeoTIFF is a terrain raster, not a native ORD Terrain Model or a certified survey.\n"
                        "Use ORD's installed Terrain > Create From File workflow if GeoTIFF is supported in your build; otherwise use an approved GIS conversion.\n"
                        "Set/verify the import elevation units; compare known control and metadata before project use.\n"
                        "Raster attachment/terrain creation are not automated by this VBA tool.\n", encoding="utf-8")
                atomic_json(job.folder / "report.json", report)
                job.state = report["state"]
                job.status("DEM verified: terrain.tif and report.json ready for review." if config["dataset"] != "lidar" else report["warning"], True)
                return report
            except Exception as error:
                report["state"] = "cancelled" if isinstance(error, InterruptedError) else "failed"
                report["error"] = str(error)
                atomic_json(job.folder / "report.json", report)
                raise


        def main():
            require(len(sys.argv) == 2, "Usage: python main.py request.json")
            request = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()
            job = Job(request.parent)
            try:
                require(request.is_file() and request.stat().st_size <= 4*1024*1024, "Missing/oversized request.json")
                config = json.loads(request.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
                run(config, job)
                return 0
            except Exception as error:
                job.state = "cancelled" if isinstance(error, InterruptedError) else "failed"
                job.status(str(error), True)
                (job.folder / "error.log").write_text(traceback.format_exc(), encoding="utf-8")
                return 2


        if __name__ == "__main__":
            sys.exit(main())
        PY
        cat > USGSTerrainDownloader/python/launch_worker.ps1 <<'PS'
        param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Request)
        $ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
        # Dedicated child environment: ORD's environment and configuration are never modified.
        try {
            $requestPath = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Request)
            $cfg = Get-Content -LiteralPath $requestPath -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
            if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $cfg.python_executable -PathType Leaf)) { throw 'Python executable not found. Configure a full python.exe path.' }
            if ([IO.Path]::GetExtension($cfg.python_executable) -ne '.exe') { throw 'Select python.exe, not a .bat/.cmd launcher.' }
            $start = New-Object Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
            $start.FileName = $cfg.python_executable
            $script = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'main.py'
            if ($script.Contains('"') -or $requestPath.Contains('"')) { throw 'Invalid quote in path.' }
            $start.Arguments = '"' + $script + '" "' + $requestPath + '"'
            $start.UseShellExecute = $false
            $start.CreateNoWindow = $true
            $start.WorkingDirectory = [IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($requestPath)
            if ($cfg.environment_file) {
                $envCfg = Get-Content -LiteralPath $cfg.environment_file -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
                foreach ($entry in $envCfg.PSObject.Properties) {
                    if ($entry.Name -eq 'PATH_prepend') {
                        $start.EnvironmentVariables['PATH'] = [string]$entry.Value + ';' + $start.EnvironmentVariables['PATH']
                    } elseif ($entry.Name -in @('GDAL_DATA','PROJ_DATA','PYTHONHOME','PYTHONPATH','QT_PLUGIN_PATH')) {
                        $start.EnvironmentVariables[$entry.Name] = [string]$entry.Value
                    } else { throw ('Unsupported child environment key: ' + $entry.Name) }
                }
            }
            $start.EnvironmentVariables['PROJ_NETWORK'] = 'OFF'
            $process = [Diagnostics.Process]::Start($start)
            $process.WaitForExit()
            exit $process.ExitCode
        } catch {
            $folder = Split-Path -Parent $Request
            $message = $_.Exception.Message
            [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $folder 'status.txt'), "failed`r`n$message", [Text.Encoding]::Unicode)
            [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $folder 'error.log'), ($_ | Out-String))
            exit 2
        }

        Attribute VB_Name = "USGSTerrainDownloaderMain"
        Option Explicit

        Private mModel As ModelReference
        Private mPicker As clsTerrainBoundaryPicker
        Private mBoundary As String
        Private mOpen As Boolean

        Public Sub Main()
            On Error GoTo Failed
            If mOpen Then
                frmUSGSTerrainDownloader.Show vbModeless
                Exit Sub
            End If
            TerrainRequire ActiveModelReference.Type = msdModelTypeNormal, "Open an active design model, not a sheet or drawing model."
            Set mModel = ActiveModelReference
            mBoundary = "": mOpen = True
            frmUSGSTerrainDownloader.Show vbModeless
            Exit Sub
        Failed:
            MsgBox Err.Description, vbExclamation, "USGS Terrain Downloader"
        End Sub

        Public Sub TerrainRequire(ByVal condition As Boolean, ByVal message As String)
            If Not condition Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2801, "USGS Terrain Downloader", message
        End Sub

        Public Sub TerrainCheckContext()
            TerrainRequire mOpen And Not mModel Is Nothing, "Start the terrain downloader again."
            TerrainRequire mModel.IsActive, "The active model changed. Close this dialog and start again in the intended model."
        End Sub

        Public Function TerrainBoundaryJSON() As String
            TerrainCheckContext
            TerrainRequire Len(mBoundary) > 0, "Draw a rectangle or select a closed straight-sided shape first."
            TerrainBoundaryJSON = mBoundary
        End Function

        Public Sub TerrainPick(ByVal rectangle As Boolean)
            On Error GoTo Failed
            TerrainCheckContext
            Set mPicker = New clsTerrainBoundaryPicker
            frmUSGSTerrainDownloader.Hide
            If rectangle Then
                CommandState.StartPrimitive mPicker
            Else
                CommandState.StartLocate mPicker
            End If
            Exit Sub
        Failed:
            MsgBox Err.Description, vbExclamation, "Boundary selection"
            frmUSGSTerrainDownloader.Show vbModeless
        End Sub

        Public Sub TerrainAcceptBoundary(vertices() As Point3d)
            TerrainCheckContext
            mBoundary = TerrainVerticesJSON(vertices)
            frmUSGSTerrainDownloader.BoundaryChanged "Boundary captured: " & CStr(UBound(vertices) - LBound(vertices) + 1) & " vertices in model XY master units. Search calculates geographic extent."
        End Sub

        Public Sub TerrainPickerEnded()
            Set mPicker = Nothing
            If mOpen Then frmUSGSTerrainDownloader.Show vbModeless
        End Sub

        Public Sub TerrainClose()
            mOpen = False
            If Not mPicker Is Nothing Then mPicker.StopPick
            Set mPicker = Nothing
            Set mModel = Nothing
            mBoundary = ""
        End Sub

        cat > USGSTerrainDownloader/vba/modTerrainCoordinates.bas <<'VB'
        Attribute VB_Name = "modTerrainCoordinates"
        Option Explicit

        Public Function TerrainNumber(ByVal value As Double) As String
            ' Str$ uses a period even on decimal-comma Windows installations.
            TerrainNumber = Trim$(Str$(value))
        End Function

        Public Function TerrainVerticesJSON(vertices() As Point3d) As String
            Dim i As Long, result As String, tolerance As Double
            TerrainRequire UBound(vertices) - LBound(vertices) >= 2, "Boundary needs three corners."
            TerrainRequire UBound(vertices) - LBound(vertices) < 20000, "Too many boundary vertices."
            tolerance = 2# / ActiveModelReference.UORsPerMasterUnit
            result = "["
            For i = LBound(vertices) To UBound(vertices)
                TerrainRequire Abs(vertices(i).Z - vertices(LBound(vertices)).Z) <= tolerance, "Select a horizontal shape. Tilted/nonplanar boundaries are unsupported."
                If i > LBound(vertices) Then result = result & ","
                result = result & "[" & TerrainNumber(vertices(i).X) & "," & TerrainNumber(vertices(i).Y) & "]"
            Next i
            TerrainVerticesJSON = result & "]"
        End Function

        Public Function TerrainModelDescription() As String
            Dim master As MeasurementUnit
            master = ActiveModelReference.MasterUnit
            TerrainModelDescription = "Model: " & ActiveModelReference.Name & " | master label: " & master.Label & _
                " | UOR/master: " & TerrainNumber(ActiveModelReference.UORsPerMasterUnit)
        End Function

        Public Function TerrainDetectedGCS() As String
            Dim model As Object, gcs As Object
            On Error GoTo Unavailable
            Set model = ActiveModelReference
            ' Bentley COM GetGCS(True) reads the model GCS, not a guessed EPSG mapping.
            Set gcs = CallByName(model, "GetGCS", VbMethod, True)
            If gcs Is Nothing Then GoTo Unavailable
            TerrainDetectedGCS = CStr(CallByName(gcs, "Name", VbGet))
            If Len(TerrainDetectedGCS) = 0 Then GoTo Unavailable
            TerrainDetectedGCS = "Bentley GCS name: " & TerrainDetectedGCS & ". Confirm equivalent EPSG/WKT and model units below."
            Exit Function
        Unavailable:
            TerrainDetectedGCS = "DGN GCS unavailable through COM. Explicit, verified EPSG/WKT and master units are REQUIRED."
        End Function
        VB
        cat > USGSTerrainDownloader/vba/clsTerrainBoundaryPicker.cls <<'VB'
        VERSION 1.0 CLASS
        BEGIN
          MultiUse = -1
        END
        Attribute VB_Name = "clsTerrainBoundaryPicker"
        Attribute VB_GlobalNameSpace = False
        Attribute VB_Creatable = False
        Attribute VB_PredeclaredId = False
        Attribute VB_Exposed = False
        Option Explicit
        Implements IPrimitiveCommandEvents
        Implements ILocateCommandEvents

        Private mFirst As Point3d
        Private mHaveFirst As Boolean
        Private mFinishing As Boolean

        Private Sub RectangleVertices(last As Point3d, vertices() As Point3d)
            ReDim vertices(0 To 4)
            vertices(0) = mFirst
            vertices(1) = Point3dFromXYZ(last.X, mFirst.Y, mFirst.Z)
            vertices(2) = Point3dFromXYZ(last.X, last.Y, mFirst.Z)
            vertices(3) = Point3dFromXYZ(mFirst.X, last.Y, mFirst.Z)
            vertices(4) = mFirst
        End Sub

        Private Sub IPrimitiveCommandEvents_Start()
            ShowCommand "USGS Terrain Downloader - Rectangle"
            ShowPrompt "Pick first corner. Rectangle follows model XY, even in a rotated view. Reset cancels."
            CommandState.EnableAccuSnap
        End Sub

        Private Sub IPrimitiveCommandEvents_DataPoint(Point As Point3d, ByVal View As View)
            Dim vertices() As Point3d, tolerance As Double
            On Error GoTo Failed
            TerrainCheckContext
            If Not mHaveFirst Then
                mFirst = Point: mHaveFirst = True
                ShowPrompt "Pick opposite corner. Preview is temporary; no DGN element will be added."
                CommandState.StartDynamics
            Else
                tolerance = 2# / ActiveModelReference.UORsPerMasterUnit
                TerrainRequire Abs(Point.X - mFirst.X) > tolerance And Abs(Point.Y - mFirst.Y) > tolerance, "Rectangle needs positive XY width and height."
                RectangleVertices Point, vertices
                TerrainAcceptBoundary vertices
                Finish
            End If
            Exit Sub
        Failed:
            ShowPrompt Err.Description
        End Sub

        Private Sub IPrimitiveCommandEvents_Dynamics(Point As Point3d, ByVal View As View, ByVal DrawMode As MsdDrawingMode)
            Dim vertices() As Point3d, preview As LineElement
            On Error GoTo Failed
            TerrainCheckContext
            If Not mHaveFirst Then Exit Sub
            RectangleVertices Point, vertices
            Set preview = CreateLineElement1(Nothing, vertices)
            preview.Redraw DrawMode
            Exit Sub
        Failed:
            ShowPrompt Err.Description
        End Sub

        Public Sub StopPick()
            mFinishing = True
            CommandState.StartDefaultCommand
        End Sub

        Private Sub Finish()
            StopPick
            TerrainPickerEnded
        End Sub

        Private Sub IPrimitiveCommandEvents_Reset()
            Finish
        End Sub
        Private Sub IPrimitiveCommandEvents_Cleanup()
            If Not mFinishing Then TerrainPickerEnded
        End Sub
        Private Sub IPrimitiveCommandEvents_Keyin(ByVal Keyin As String)
        End Sub

        Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_Start()
            Dim criteria As LocateCriteria
            Set criteria = CommandState.CreateLocateCriteria(False)
            CommandState.SetLocateCriteria criteria
            ShowCommand "USGS Terrain Downloader - Closed Shape"
            ShowPrompt "Identify a straight-sided shape in the active model, then accept. Reset cancels."
        End Sub

        Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_LocateFilter(ByVal Element As Element, Point As Point3d, Accepted As Boolean)
            On Error GoTo Failed
            Accepted = False
            If Element.ModelReference.IsAttachment Then Exit Sub
            If Not Element.ModelReference.IsActive Then Exit Sub
            Accepted = (Element.Type = msdElementTypeShape)
            If Not Accepted Then ShowPrompt "Select a simple closed shape. Complex/curved shapes, holes, cells and references are unsupported."
            Exit Sub
        Failed:
            Accepted = False
            ShowPrompt Err.Description
        End Sub

        Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_Accept(ByVal Element As Element, Point As Point3d, ByVal View As View)
            Dim vertices() As Point3d
            On Error GoTo Failed
            TerrainCheckContext
            TerrainRequire Element.ModelReference.IsActive And Not Element.ModelReference.IsAttachment, "Select in the active model."
            TerrainRequire Element.Type = msdElementTypeShape, "Only simple closed shapes are supported."
            vertices = Element.AsVertexList.GetVertices
            TerrainAcceptBoundary vertices
            Finish
            Exit Sub
        Failed:
            ShowPrompt Err.Description
        End Sub

        Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_LocateReset()
            Finish
        End Sub
        Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_LocateFailed()
            ShowPrompt "No supported shape found. Reset cancels."
        End Sub
        Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_Dynamics(Point As Point3d, ByVal View As View, ByVal DrawMode As MsdDrawingMode)
        End Sub
        Private Sub ILocateCommandEvents_Cleanup()
            If Not mFinishing Then TerrainPickerEnded
        End Sub

        cat > USGSTerrainDownloader/vba/modPythonBridge.bas <<'VB'
        Attribute VB_Name = "modPythonBridge"
        Option Explicit

        #If VBA7 Then
        Private Declare PtrSafe Sub Sleep Lib "kernel32" (ByVal milliseconds As Long)
        #Else
        Private Declare Sub Sleep Lib "kernel32" (ByVal milliseconds As Long)
        #End If

        Public Function TerrainQuoteJSON(ByVal value As String) As String
            Dim i As Long, code As Long, ch As String, result As String
            result = Chr$(34)
            For i = 1 To Len(value)
                ch = Mid$(value, i, 1): code = AscW(ch)
                Select Case code
                    Case 34: result = result & "\" & Chr$(34)
                    Case 92: result = result & "\\"
                    Case 0 To 31: result = result & "\u" & Right$("0000" & Hex$(code), 4)
                    Case Else: result = result & ch
                End Select
            Next i
            TerrainQuoteJSON = result & Chr$(34)
        End Function

        Public Function TerrainBool(ByVal value As Boolean) As String
            If value Then TerrainBool = "true" Else TerrainBool = "false"
        End Function

        Public Sub TerrainWriteUTF8(ByVal path As String, ByVal text As String)
            Dim stream As Object
            Set stream = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
            stream.Type = 2: stream.Charset = "utf-8": stream.Open
            stream.WriteText text
            stream.SaveToFile path, 2
            stream.Close
        End Sub

        Public Function TerrainReadUI(ByVal path As String) As String
            Dim fs As Object, stream As Object
            Set fs = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
            If Not fs.FileExists(path) Then Exit Function
            Set stream = fs.OpenTextFile(path, 1, False, -1)
            TerrainReadUI = stream.ReadAll
            stream.Close
        End Function

        Public Function TerrainNewJobFolder(ByVal destination As String) As String
            Dim fs As Object, path As String
            Set fs = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
            TerrainRequire fs.FolderExists(destination), "Destination must be an existing writable folder. Browse or create it first."
            path = fs.BuildPath(destination, "run-" & Format$(Now, "yyyymmdd-hhnnss") & "-" & fs.GetTempName)
            fs.CreateFolder path
            TerrainNewJobFolder = path
        End Function

        Private Function CommandQuote(ByVal value As String) As String
            TerrainRequire InStr(value, Chr$(34)) = 0 And InStr(value, vbCr) = 0 And InStr(value, vbLf) = 0, "Invalid command path."
            TerrainRequire Right$(value, 1) <> "\", "Command argument cannot end in a slash."
            CommandQuote = Chr$(34) & value & Chr$(34)
        End Function

        Public Function TerrainStartWorker(ByVal sourceRoot As String, ByVal requestPath As String) As Object
            Dim shell As Object, fs As Object, launcher As String, exe As String
            Set fs = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
            launcher = fs.BuildPath(sourceRoot, "python\launch_worker.ps1")
            TerrainRequire fs.FileExists(launcher), "Source folder must contain python\launch_worker.ps1."
            exe = Environ$("SystemRoot") & "\System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe"
            Set shell = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
            ' -File receives data arguments directly, no cmd.exe or interpolated PowerShell expressions.
            Set TerrainStartWorker = shell.Exec(CommandQuote(exe) & " -NoProfile -NonInteractive -File " & CommandQuote(launcher) & " -Request " & CommandQuote(requestPath))
        End Function

        Public Sub TerrainYield()
            DoEvents
            Sleep 100
        End Sub

        Public Sub TerrainCancelJob(ByVal folder As String)
            If Len(folder) > 0 Then TerrainWriteUTF8 folder & "\cancel.flag", "cancel"
        End Sub

        Public Function TerrainBrowseFolder(ByVal title As String) As String
            Dim shell As Object, folder As Object
            Set shell = CreateObject("Shell.Application")
            Set folder = shell.BrowseForFolder(0, title, 0)
            If Not folder Is Nothing Then TerrainBrowseFolder = folder.Self.path
        End Function

        Public Sub TerrainOpenFolder(ByVal path As String)
            Dim shell As Object, fs As Object
            Set fs = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
            TerrainRequire fs.FolderExists(path), "Folder does not exist."
            Set shell = CreateObject("Shell.Application")
            shell.Explore path
        End Sub
        VB

## Assistant

The DEM pipeline now separates the horizontal transform from elevation scaling and checks source grids, vertical declarations, and coverage gaps. I found a usable GDAL/PROJ runtime bundled with QGIS on this Mac, so I can test it with tiny synthetic rasters. The VBA form and file-based worker bridge are next.

        Public Sub BoundaryChanged(ByVal message As String)
            mSearchFolder = "": lstProducts.Clear
            lblExtent.caption = message: lblTotals.caption = "Search required for new boundary."
        End Sub

        Private Sub cmdRectangle_Click()
            If Not mBusy Then TerrainPick True
        End Sub
        Private Sub cmdShape_Click()
            If Not mBusy Then TerrainPick False
        End Sub
        Private Sub cmdBrowse_Click()
            Dim path As String
            path = TerrainBrowseFolder("Choose a writable project terrain-data folder")
            If Len(path) > 0 Then txtOutput.value = path
        End Sub

        Private Function DatasetKey() As String
            Select Case cboDataset.ListIndex
                Case 0: DatasetKey = "dem1m"
                Case 1: DatasetKey = "dem13"
                Case 2: DatasetKey = "lidar"
                Case Else: TerrainRequire False, "Select a USGS dataset."
            End Select
        End Function

        Private Function RequestJSON(ByVal action As String) As String
            Dim result As String, ids As String, i As Long, fields As Variant, cap As Double, resolution As Double
            TerrainCheckContext
            TerrainRequire chkConfirmed.value And Len(Trim$(txtCRS.Text)) > 0 And cboMaster.ListIndex >= 0, "Explicitly verify source CRS, model master units and grid-coordinate mapping."
            result = "{""schema_version"":1,""action"":" & TerrainQuoteJSON(action) & _
                ",""boundary"":" & TerrainBoundaryJSON() & ",""source_crs"":" & TerrainQuoteJSON(Trim$(txtCRS.Text)) & _
                ",""master_unit"":" & TerrainQuoteJSON(cboMaster.value) & ",""crs_confirmed"":true,""coordinates_are_grid"":true" & _
                ",""dataset"":" & TerrainQuoteJSON(DatasetKey()) & ",""python_executable"":" & TerrainQuoteJSON(Trim$(txtPython.Text)) & _
                ",""environment_file"":" & TerrainQuoteJSON(Trim$(txtEnv.Text)) & ",""dgn_context"":" & TerrainQuoteJSON(TerrainModelDescription())
            If action = "process" Then
                TerrainRequire Len(mSearchFolder) > 0, "Search USGS first."
                For i = 0 To lstProducts.ListCount - 1
                    If lstProducts.Selected(i) Then
                        fields = Split(mRows(i), vbTab)
                        If Len(ids) > 0 Then ids = ids & ","
                        ids = ids & TerrainQuoteJSON(CStr(fields(0)))
                    End If
                Next i
                TerrainRequire Len(ids) > 0, "Select at least one product."
                TerrainRequire IsNumeric(txtCap.Text), "Download cap must be numeric GiB."
                cap = CDbl(txtCap.Text)
                TerrainRequire cap > 0 And cap <= 100, "Download cap must be > 0 and <= 100 GiB."
                result = result & ",""selected_ids"": [" & ids & "],""search_manifest"":" & TerrainQuoteJSON(mSearchFolder & "\search.json") & _
                    ",""max_download_bytes"":" & Format$(Fix(cap * 1073741824#), "0") & _
                    ",""unknown_size_confirmed"":" & TerrainBool(chkUnknown.value)
                If DatasetKey() <> "lidar" Then
                    TerrainRequire cboSourceZ.ListIndex >= 0 And Len(Trim$(txtVertical.Text)) > 0 And Len(Trim$(txtEvidence.Text)) >= 12, "Verify selected sources' vertical datum/unit and enter source metadata evidence. Mixed datums are unsupported."
                    TerrainRequire IsNumeric(txtResolution.Text), "Cell size must be numeric in source CRS native horizontal units."
                    resolution = CDbl(txtResolution.Text)
                    TerrainRequire resolution > 0, "Cell size must be positive."
                    result = result & ",""source_vertical"": {""unit"":" & TerrainQuoteJSON(cboSourceZ.value) & _
                        ",""crs"":" & TerrainQuoteJSON(Trim$(txtVertical.Text)) & ",""evidence"":" & TerrainQuoteJSON(Trim$(txtEvidence.Text)) & "}" & _
                        ",""resolution"":" & TerrainNumber(resolution) & ",""elevation_unit"":" & TerrainQuoteJSON(cboOutput.value) & _
                        ",""clip"":" & TerrainBool(chkClip.value) & ",""overlap_order_confirmed"":" & TerrainBool(chkOverlap.value) & _
                        ",""allow_gaps"":" & TerrainBool(chkGaps.value)
                End If
            End If
            RequestJSON = result & "}"
        End Function

        Private Sub SetBusy(ByVal busy As Boolean)
            Dim control As MSForms.Control
            mBusy = busy
            For Each control In Me.Controls
                If TypeName(control) <> "Label" Then control.Enabled = Not busy
            Next control
            cmdCancel.Enabled = True
        End Sub

        Private Sub RunWorker(ByVal action As String)
            Dim request As String, worker As Object, text As String, errorText As String
            On Error GoTo Failed
            If mBusy Then Exit Sub
            request = RequestJSON(action)
            mJob = TerrainNewJobFolder(Trim$(txtOutput.Text)): mLastFolder = mJob
            TerrainWriteUTF8 mJob & "\request.json", request
            If action = "search" Then mSearchFolder = "": lstProducts.Clear
            SetBusy True
            lblStatus.caption = "Starting " & action & "..."
            Set worker = TerrainStartWorker(Trim$(txtRoot.Text), mJob & "\request.json")
            Do While worker.status = 0
                text = TerrainReadUI(mJob & "\status.txt")
                If Len(text) > 0 Then lblStatus.caption = Replace(text, vbCrLf, ": ")
                TerrainYield
            Loop
            text = TerrainReadUI(mJob & "\status.txt")
            If Len(text) > 0 Then lblStatus.caption = Replace(text, vbCrLf, ": ")
            TerrainRequire worker.ExitCode = 0, "Worker failed/cancelled. " & text & vbCrLf & "See " & mJob & "\error.log. If Python did not start, verify environment paths and PowerShell policy."
            If action = "search" Then
                mSearchFolder = mJob
                LoadProducts
                lblExtent.caption = TerrainReadUI(mJob & "\extent.txt")
            End If
            SetBusy False
            Exit Sub
        Failed:
            errorText = Err.Description
            On Error Resume Next
            If Not worker Is Nothing Then
                If worker.status = 0 Then
                    TerrainCancelJob mJob
                    Do While worker.status = 0
                        TerrainYield
                    Loop
                End If
            End If
            On Error GoTo 0
            SetBusy False
            lblStatus.caption = errorText
            MsgBox errorText, vbExclamation, "USGS Terrain Downloader"
        End Sub

        Private Sub LoadProducts()
            Dim contents As String, i As Long, fields As Variant, size As String
            lstProducts.Clear
            contents = TerrainReadUI(mSearchFolder & "\products.tsv")
            If Len(contents) = 0 Then Exit Sub
            mRows = Split(contents, vbCrLf)
            For i = 0 To UBound(mRows)
                fields = Split(mRows(i), vbTab)
                TerrainRequire UBound(fields) = 8, "Invalid products.tsv row."
                size = "unknown"
                If IsNumeric(fields(2)) Then size = Format$(CDbl(fields(2)) / 1048576#, "0.0")
                lstProducts.AddItem fields(1)
                lstProducts.List(i, 1) = fields(4): lstProducts.List(i, 2) = fields(3): lstProducts.List(i, 3) = size
            Next i
            RefreshTotals
        End Sub

        Private Sub RefreshTotals()
            Dim i As Long, count As Long, total As Double, unknown As Long, fields As Variant
            For i = 0 To lstProducts.ListCount - 1
                If lstProducts.Selected(i) Then
                    count = count + 1: fields = Split(mRows(i), vbTab)
                    If IsNumeric(fields(2)) Then total = total + CDbl(fields(2)) Else unknown = unknown + 1
                End If
            Next i
            lblTotals.caption = CStr(count) & " / " & CStr(lstProducts.ListCount) & " selected; known estimate " & Format$(total / 1073741824#, "0.000") & " GiB; " & CStr(unknown) & " unknown sizes."
        End Sub

        Private Sub lstProducts_Change()
            Dim fields As Variant
            If lstProducts.ListIndex >= 0 Then
                fields = Split(mRows(lstProducts.ListIndex), vbTab)
                txtDetails.value = "Coverage: " & fields(5) & vbCrLf & "Metadata: " & fields(7) & vbCrLf & fields(8)
            End If
            RefreshTotals
        End Sub
        Private Sub cmdSearch_Click()
            RunWorker "search"
        End Sub
        Private Sub cmdProcess_Click()
            RunWorker "process"
        End Sub
        Private Sub cmdOpen_Click()
            On Error GoTo Failed
            If Len(mLastFolder) > 0 Then TerrainOpenFolder mLastFolder Else TerrainOpenFolder txtOutput.Text
            Exit Sub
        Failed:
            MsgBox Err.Description, vbExclamation, "Output folder"
        End Sub
        Private Sub cmdCancel_Click()
            If mBusy Then
                TerrainCancelJob mJob
                lblStatus.caption = "Cancellation requested. Waiting for current network/GDAL operation to stop..."
            Else
                Unload Me
            End If
        End Sub
        Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)
            If mBusy Then
                Cancel = True
                TerrainCancelJob mJob
                lblStatus.caption = "Cancellation requested. Dialog will remain until worker stops."
            Else
                TerrainClose
            End If
        End Sub

    $ cat > USGSTerrainDownloader/tests/test_crs.py <<'PY'
        import math
        import sys
        import unittest
        from pathlib import Path
        sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'python'))
        from pyproj import CRS, Transformer
        from crs_utils import prepare_boundary, linear_factor, densify, ring_valid


        def request(epsg=26916):
            x, y = Transformer.from_crs(4326, epsg, always_xy=True).transform(-88.81, 33.43)
            return {'schema_version': 1, 'crs_confirmed': True, 'coordinates_are_grid': True,
                    'source_crs': f'EPSG:{epsg}', 'master_unit': 'm',
                    'boundary': [[x,y], [x+100,y], [x+100,y+100], [x,y+100]]}


        class CRSTests(unittest.TestCase):
            def test_unit_definitions(self):
                self.assertEqual(linear_factor('ft','m'), 0.3048)
                self.assertEqual(linear_factor('us-ft','m'), 1200/3937)
                self.assertNotEqual(linear_factor('ft','m'), linear_factor('us-ft','m'))
                self.assertAlmostEqual(100 * linear_factor('m','us-ft'), 328.0833333333333)
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError): linear_factor('feet','m')

            def test_mississippi_crs_and_master_conversion(self):
                # EPSG database definitions, not a zone inferred from numeric coordinates.
                for epsg in (26994,26995,2254,2255,6507,6508,6509,6510):
                    crs = CRS.from_epsg(epsg)
                    config = request(epsg)
                    native = crs.axis_info[0].unit_conversion_factor
                    config['master_unit'] = 'us-ft' if abs(native-1200/3937) < 1e-12 else 'm'
                    b = prepare_boundary(config)['bbox']
                    self.assertLess(b[0], -88.81); self.assertGreater(b[2], -88.81)
                config = request(2254)
                config['master_unit'] = 'ft'
                # Feed master international-foot coordinates corresponding to EPSG survey feet.
                ratio = (1200/3937)/0.3048
                config['boundary'] = [[x*ratio,y*ratio] for x,y in config['boundary']]
                self.assertAlmostEqual(prepare_boundary(config)['model_to_native_scale'], 0.999998)

            def test_confirmation_and_reasonableness(self):
                for key in ('crs_confirmed','coordinates_are_grid'):
                    c=request(); c[key]=False
                    with self.assertRaises(ValueError): prepare_boundary(c)
                for ring in ([[0,0],[1,1],[2,2]], [[0,0],[1,0],[math.nan,1]]):
                    c=request(); c['boundary']=ring
                    with self.assertRaises(ValueError): prepare_boundary(c)
                c=request(); c['source_crs']='EPSG:4326'
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError): prepare_boundary(c)
                c=request(); c['boundary']=[[0,0],[10,0],[10,10],[0,10]]
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError): prepare_boundary(c)

            def test_densifies_all_edges(self):
                ring=ring_valid([[0,0],[1000,0],[1000,1000],[0,1000]])
                dense=densify(ring,1)
                self.assertEqual(len(dense),41)
                self.assertEqual(dense[0],dense[-1])
                self.assertTrue(all(math.dist(a,b)<=100 for a,b in zip(dense,dense[1:])))

            def test_bbox_contains_independent_dense_probes(self):
                c=request(26916)
                x,y=c['boundary'][0]; c['boundary']=[[x,y],[x+10000,y],[x+10000,y+10000],[x,y+10000]]
                b=prepare_boundary(c)['bbox']
                transform=Transformer.from_crs(c['source_crs'],4326,always_xy=True)
                points=densify(ring_valid(c['boundary']),1,segment_m=7)
                for x,y in points:
                    lon,lat=transform.transform(x,y)
                    self.assertTrue(b[0]<=lon<=b[2] and b[1]<=lat<=b[3])
